Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于：（1）提出PelviNeXt这一跨模态、无需任务特定调整的通用盆腔影像架构，证明单一架构可在数据稀缺且研究不足的女性健康领域充当可靠基础模型；（2）通过严谨的数据完整性审计揭示了公开数据集中的重复污染问题并发布去重版本，促进了评估的公平性和可复现性。局限性在于：两个任务都仅依赖单一公开数据集，跨数据集泛化能力尚未验证；数据规模仍然有限，可能限制架构在大规模数据下的表现评估。

Abstract: Women's health remains substantially under-resourced in medical imaging research, with pelvic pathologies such as polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) and pelvic fracture both suffering from a scarcity of public, well-annotated benchmark data despite their clinical importance. We introduce PelviNeXt, a modality-agnostic hybrid architecture combining a dense convolutional feature extractor, hierarchical channel-spatial attention (H-CBAM), a multi-scale fusion module (MSFM), and talking-heads multi-head self-attention (TH-MHSA), applied without modification to both pelvic ultrasound and X-ray inputs. While benchmarking PelviNeXt on PCOSGen, the only gynaecologist-annotated public PCOS ultrasound dataset, we identified extensive exact and near-duplicate contamination within and across the dataset. We audit this contamination via perceptual hashing, publicly release a deduplicated version of the dataset, and establish the first integrity-audited evaluation protocol and baseline for PCOSGen under 5-fold cross-validation. On the only publicly available pelvic fracture X-ray dataset (PXR150), PelviNeXt exceeds previously reported state-of-the-art results across accuracy, recall, specificity, and AUROC. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component contributes to performance on both tasks. Our results demonstrate that a single architecture, applied without task-specific modification, can serve as a reliable foundation for pelvic imaging across modalities in data-scarce, under-researched areas of women's health.

</details>


### [55] [Artificial Intelligence for Workflow Analysis in Colorectal Surgery: A Multicentric, Cross-Procedural Development and Generalization Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20154)
*Pietro Mascagni,Julia Alekseenko,Pooja P Jain,Marta Goglia,Andrea Balla,Ludovica Baldari,Gianfranco Silecchia,Claudio Fiorillo,Vincenzo Tondolo,Salvador Morales-Conde,Luigi Boni,Sergio Alfieri,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI-ColoWorkflow，一个用于微创结直肠手术（MIS-CRS）视频自动化工作流分析的深度学习模型。该模型由微调后的 DINOv3 视觉Transformer（用于逐帧视觉特征提取）与分层多阶段时序卷积网络联合优化而成，在多个中心的合并数据集上训练，用于阶段（phase）和步骤（step）的自动识别，并系统比较了全局模型与中心特定/术式特定模型的泛化性能。阶段识别宏F1达73.01%，步骤识别为39.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIS-CRS具有显著的手术变异性与不一致的临床结局。虽然 ColoWorkflow 工具已被验证可用于此类手术的视频工作流评估（VBA），但人工VBA耗时费力，严重制约其临床落地与规模化应用。视频数据和逐帧语义标注需要大量专家时间，因此亟需自动化深度学习方法来自动识别手术阶段与步骤，以提升手术质量评估的效率和一致性。此外，以往手术AI模型多为单中心或单术式开发，其跨中心、跨术式泛化能力不明，本文针对这一核心问题展开系统研究。

Method: 方法核心包含两方面创新性设计：(1) 视觉特征提取采用微调的 DINOv3 视觉Transformer对每一帧进行特征提取，利用大规模自监督预训练的表征能力捕捉手术场景中的细粒度视觉语义；(2) 时序建模采用分层多阶段时序卷积网络（hierarchical multi-stage temporal convolutional network），对帧级特征进行联合优化，同时完成阶段（phase）和步骤（step）两个粒度的识别任务。模型在来自4个中心加公开数据集的合并多中心数据上端到端联合训练得到全局模型 AI-ColoWorkflow，并与中心特定（centre-specific）和术式特定（procedure-specific）的单源训练模型在独立留出测试集上进行对比。评估指标包括宏F1、平衡准确率、精确率和召回率。

Result: 在留出测试集上，AI-ColoWorkflow阶段识别宏F1为73.01%±10.27（平衡准确率73.43%），步骤识别宏F1为39.82%±7.06（平衡准确率38.65%）。全局模型在大多数实验中优于中心特定和术式特定模型，唯术式特定的步骤识别除外。泛化分析中阶段识别的平均F1为48.42%。结果表明：在合并的多中心、多术式数据上训练的单一模型，其阶段识别泛化性能至少不逊于且通常优于中心/术式特定模型；而术式特定模型在部分术式的步骤识别上仍具优势，从而支持未来手术AI采用混合训练策略。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：首次系统验证了合并多中心、多术式数据训练的统一模型在MIS-CRS工作流自动化分析中的可行性与泛化能力，证明了AI-ColoWorkflow能可靠识别MIS-CRS阶段，为手术质量评估的自动化落地提供了有力证据。其阶段识别已具临床可用性，但步骤识别F1较低（39.82%），识别难度显著更高，是需要改进的方向。术式特定与全局模型的性能差异提示混合训练策略的价值。局限包括：步骤识别精度不足、样本不平衡影响较大、以及模型在推广到其他手术中心时的泛化仍待更大规模验证。

Abstract: Minimally invasive colorectal surgeries (MIS-CRS) are characterised by significant variability and inconsistent outcomes. ColoWorkflow, a tool for the video-based assessment (VBA) of MIS-CRS workflow, was recently validated. However, manual VBA is time-consuming, limiting implementation. This study presents AI-ColoWorkflow, a deep learning model for automated surgical workflow analysis across MIS-CRS. Operative videos of MIS-CRS were collected from 4 centres and a publicly available dataset. Phases and steps were manually annotated according to ColoWorkflow. A deep learning model combining a fine-tuned DINOv3 vision transformer for per-frame visual feature extraction with a hierarchical multi-stage temporal convolutional network was jointly optimized for phase and step recognition. The model trained on pooled multicentric data, namely AI-ColoWorkflow was compared against centre-specific and procedure-specific models on a held-out test set. The following metrics were used for evaluation: macro F1 score, balanced accuracy, precision, and recall. AI-ColoWorkflow achieved a macro F1 of 73.01% $\pm$ 10.27 (balanced accuracy 73.43%) for phase recognition and 39.82% $\pm$ 7.06 (balanced accuracy 38.65%) for step recognition. The global model outperformed centre- and procedure-specific models in most experiments except procedure-specific step recognition. In the generalization analysis, mean F1 was 48.42% for phase recognition. AI-ColoWorkflow can reliably recognize MIS-CRS phases. A single model trained on pooled, multicentric, multi-procedural data generalises at least as well as and often better than centre- or procedure-specific models for phase recognition in MIS-CRS, while procedure-specific step models retain advantages for certain procedure types, motivating hybrid training strategies for future surgical AI development.

</details>


### [56] [G3Ego: Gaze-Guided Graphs for Egocentric Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20157)
*Marko Haralović,Akash Ramakrishnan,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G3Ego 提出了一种基于图结构的自我中心（egocentric）动作理解框架，将注视（gaze）作为结构线索直接融入动作场景图的构建中，而非仅作为辅助模态或注意力信号。通过从稀疏采样帧中构建场景图（利用视觉-语言描述、目标 grounding 和手部线索），并利用穿戴者注视剪除无关实体，G3Ego 生成高效、可解释的图嵌入用于动作识别与预测，在 EGTEA Gaze+ 和 MECCANO 数据集上达到与视频方法竞争力相当的性能，且避免了昂贵的视频预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前自我中心动作理解通常依赖在大规模外心（exocentric）数据集上预训练的大型视频模型。但第一人称动作往往主要由少数关键的手-物交互决定，只涉及少量相关实体，视频模型因此存在计算开销大、冗余信息多的问题。现有方法将注视仅用作辅助模态或注意力信号，未充分利用注视作为"穿戴者意图指示器"的结构性价值。作者旨在利用注视筛选动作相关实体，构建高效且可解释的动作表示，从而避免对昂贵视频预训练的依赖。

Method: G3Ego 的核心创新是将注视引入图构建而非仅作注意力信号。具体流程：(1) 从稀疏采样帧出发，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）生成场景描述，并 ground 出相关目标与手部线索；(2) 构建"动作场景图"（action scene graph），其节点为场景实体、边为手-物/物-物交互关系；(3) 使用相机穿戴者的注视信息剪除图中与动作无关的实体，从而聚焦于动作相关的少量交互；(4) 对剪枝后的图嵌入进行时间聚合，用于动作识别（recognition）与动作预测（anticipation）。该方法的核心技术贡献在于图结构 + 注视剪枝的组合，产生紧凑、可解释且不依赖大规模视频预训练的动作表示。

Result: 实验在 EGTEA Gaze+ 和 MECCANO 两个自我中心动作数据集上评估，采用动作识别与动作预测两种任务，指标包括（尤其在类别不平衡下的）Macro-F1 等。结果表明 G3Ego 与基于视频的方法相比取得具有竞争力的性能，并在类别不平衡评估中一致提升 Macro-F1；同时避免了计算开销大的视频预训练，验证了注视引导图表示在自我中心动作理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: G3Ego 的主要贡献在于首次将注视作为结构线索（而非辅助注意力信号）直接融入动作场景图构建，实现面向动作相关交互的高效、可解释表示，并在识别与预测任务上达到与视频方法接近的效果。局限性包括：依赖于 VLM 的场景描述与目标 grounding 质量以及注视数据的可用性（部分场景可能缺少注视标注），图构建的文本管线可能引入额外延迟，相关方法与纯端到端视频模型的可扩展性和定性分析有待进一步探讨。

Abstract: Egocentric action understanding is often addressed using large video models pretrained on extensive exocentric datasets. However, many first-person actions depend on a small number of hand-object interactions involving only a few relevant entities.
  We propose G3Ego, a graph-based framework for egocentric action understanding that uses gaze as a structural cue to identify action-relevant entities in the scene. From sparsely sampled frames, G3Ego constructs action scene graphs from vision-language descriptions, grounded objects, and hand cues, and then prunes irrelevant entities using the camera wearer's gaze.
  The resulting graph embeddings are temporally aggregated for action recognition and anticipation. Unlike prior work that uses gaze primarily as an auxiliary modality or attention signal, G3Ego incorporates gaze directly into graph construction, producing efficient and interpretable representations focused on action-relevant interactions.
  Experiments on EGTEA Gaze+ and MECCANO show that G3Ego achieves competitive performance compared with video-based approaches and consistently improves Macro-F1 under class-imbalanced evaluation, while avoiding reliance on computationally expensive video pretraining. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of gaze-guided graph representations for egocentric action understanding.

</details>


### [57] [RoMAN-Flow: Taming Autoregressive Normalizing Flows for Offline Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20208)
*Shaoxuan Wang,Guangting Zheng,Rui Huang,Zhipeng Tang,Sha Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMAN-Flow 是一个面向机器人操作的离线强化学习框架，通过采样无关的优势加权似然目标和一步式动作生成器蒸馏，解决了 AR-NF（自回归归一化流）策略在策略优化和部署阶段的双重采样瓶颈，在多个仿真和真实机器人平台实验中实现了与现有方法相当的策略性能并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习可利用先前收集的数据改进机器人策略而无需与环境交互。然而，目前主流的扩散模型（diffusion）和流匹配（flow-matching）机器人策略缺乏可处理的似然（tractable likelihood），限制了基于似然的离线 RL 后训练的应用。AR-NF 同时具备表达性动作建模和精确似然评估能力，但其自回归结构的顺序式采样在策略优化（反复采样计算优势权重）和部署阶段都带来显著计算开销，阻碍了实际应用。这一问题对于需要低延迟动作预测的机器人操作场景尤为关键。

Method: RoMAN-Flow 的核心创新在于从两个阶段应对 AR-NF 的采样瓶颈：(1) 策略优化阶段采用采样无关（sampling-free）的优势加权似然目标，直接从离线数据集中为高优势动作赋予更高似然，无需从自回归策略中采样即可完成优化；(2) 部署阶段将优化后的自回归策略蒸馏为一个单步动作生成器（one-step action generator），实现低延迟动作预测。该框架兼顾了 AR-NF 的表达能力与精确似然性质，同时通过上述两个阶段的设计大幅缩短了采样开销。

Result: 论文在多个仿真操作基准和真实机器人平台上进行实验。结果表明 RoMAN-Flow 在策略性能上与现有方法相当（competitive policy performance），同时在推理延迟（inference latency）上实现了大幅降低，验证了其解决采样瓶颈方法的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: RoMAN-Flow 的主要贡献是将 AR-NF 策略引入实际可用的机器人操作离线 RL 场景：通过采样无关的优势加权优化与一步式蒸馏，既保留了 AR-NF 的精确似然建模优势，又克服了其顺序采样的计算瓶颈，实现了性能与低延迟的兼顾。潜在局限包括实验对比的鲁棒性、极复杂操作任务下的蒸馏保真度，以及采样无关目标在优势估计噪声下的稳定性等问题，论文中未对这些方面作深入讨论。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning improves robotic policies using previously collected data without further environment interaction. Yet prevalent diffusion- and flow-matching robot policies lack tractable likelihoods, limiting their use in likelihood-based offline RL post-training. AR-NFs offer both expressive action modeling and exact likelihood evaluation, but their sequential sampling incurs substantial sampling overhead during policy optimization and deployment. We present RoMAN-Flow (Robotic Manipulation with Autoregressive Normalizing Flows), an offline reinforcement learning framework that makes AR-NF policies practical for robotic manipulation by addressing this sampling bottleneck in both stages. During policy optimization, RoMAN-Flow employs a sampling-free, advantage-weighted likelihood objective that assigns higher likelihood to high-advantage actions from the offline dataset without sampling from the autoregressive policy. For efficient deployment, it distills the optimized autoregressive policy into a one-step action generator, enabling low-latency action prediction. Experiments across multiple simulated manipulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that RoMAN-Flow achieves competitive policy performance while substantially reducing inference latency. Code is available at https://github.com/konnyaku28/RoMAN-Flow.

</details>


### [58] [Unwarping the Lens: A Physics-Grounded Approach to Video Glasses Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20212)
*Radim Spetlik,David Futschik,Radek Danecek,Feitong Tan,Ziqian Bai,Rohit Pandey,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向视频的眼镜移除高保真框架 JFSnet。该方法利用商业级生成模型（Nano Banana）提取高保真合成人脸图像，经三阶段结构滤波保持身份/表情/位姿一致性，并通过物理模拟镜片光学特性在训练中生成多样化配对数据，将生成先验的知识迁移到专用复原网络 JFSnet 中。JFSnet 融合 DINOv2 语义特征与卷积解码器，借助平移等变约束提升时序一致性。在 FFHQ 子集上实现 27.68 FPS 推理速度，并在 CelebV-Text 感知实验中优于扩散与 GAN 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼镜移除是面部属性编辑中的棘手任务，因为面部几何常被复杂的折射畸变和视角相关的高光反射所遮挡。虽然大规模生成先验在静态图像修复中表现良好，但其随机性缺乏保持身份、表情和位姿的结构约束，导致静态与动态序列中出现明显的"身份漂移"（identity drift）。该问题在视频场景中尤为突出，需要兼顾高保真度、结构保持与时序稳定性，这正是本文要解决的核心难题。

Method: 方法包含三大技术环节：(1) 提取与正则化：从商业级生成模型（Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image）中提取高保真合成人脸图像，并通过三阶段结构滤波流程保留身份、表情与位姿；(2) 数据生成：在训练中应用基于物理的镜片光学模拟，生成多样化的成对训练数据，从而将生成先验的多视角照片级先验知识迁移到专用复原架构中；(3) 复原网络 JFSnet（Joint Feature-Spatial network）：融合 DINOv2 提取的语义特征与卷积解码器进行空间重建，利用平移等变约束（translation equivariance）提升时序一致性并保留高频细节。该架构在保持生成模型多视角知识的同时间接约束结构一致性。

Result: 在精心整理的 Flickr-Faces-HQ（FFHQ）子集（12,163 张图像）上进行评估，该方法实现高保真度与结构准确性，同时保持 27.68 FPS 的推理速度，满足实时应用需求。在 CelebV-Text 视频序列的感知研究中，该方法在眼区一致性、时序稳定性及整体复原质量方面持续优于扩散和 GAN 类基线。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出一种"知识迁移"式框架，将开放生成模型（Nano Banana）的随机生成先验通过结构滤波与物理光学模拟加以约束，转化为确定性、保持身份的视频级眼镜移除能力。JFSnet 通过语义-空间特征联合与平移等变约束，兼顾了高频细节与时序稳定性，且推理速度快。潜在局限包括：依赖商业模型的生成质量、三阶段滤波的复杂度、以及 DINOv2 特征的引入带来的计算开销；且实验主要依赖感知评估，缺乏对身份保真度的定量客观指标。

Abstract: High-fidelity removal of eyeglasses from video is a major challenge in facial attribute editing, as the underlying facial geometry is often obscured by complex refractive distortions and view-dependent specular reflections. While large-scale generative priors have shown promise in eye-glasses removal via static image inpainting, they often lack the structural constraints necessary to maintain identity, expression, and pose, leading to visible "identity drift" in both static images and dynamic sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel transfer framework that addresses the stochastic nature of generative priors. Our pipeline first extracts high-fidelity synthetic face images from a commercial-grade generative model (Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image), regularizes them via a three-stage structural filtering process to preserve identity, expression, and pose, and finally applies physically-based simulation of lens optics during training to provide diverse, paired data. This process transfers Nano Banana's photo-realistic, multi-view knowledge into a specialized restoration architecture, JFSnet (Joint Feature-Spatial network). JFSnet integrates DINOv2-based semantic features with a convolutional decoder for spatial reconstruction, leveraging translation equivariance constraints to improve temporal consistency and high-frequency detail preservation. Evaluations on the curated Flickr-Faces-HQ (FFHQ) subset (12,163 images) show that our approach achieves high fidelity and structural accuracy, while maintaining inference speed of 27.68 FPS. In perceptual studies on CelebV-Text video sequences, our results are consistently preferred over diffusion and GAN-based baselines for ocular consistency, temporal stability, and overall restoration quality.

</details>


### [59] [Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention for Hierarchical Feature Modulation toward Anatomy-Agnostic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20229)
*Mosharof Hossain,Md Rabiul Islam,Limon Halder,Erchin Serpedin,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA)，一种新颖的提示调制机制，通过池化提取紧凑通道描述符、投影到共享空间、并经门控激励机制融合，生成提示感知的通道注意力权重，从而在编码器-解码器网络的多个层级实现语义提示的深度层次化整合。并在此基础上提出PROMISE-Net框架，包含卷积(PROMISE-CNN)和Transformer(PROMISE-Txformer)两种变体，在ISIC-Lesion、Kvasir-Polyp、CAMUS-Cardiac和Kvasir-Instrument四个医学图像分割基准上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖学上合理的分割任务因低对比度、模糊边界和模态特异伪影而极具挑战。交互式分割通过提示(如点击、框选)引导特征提取和定位，成为解决结构模糊区域的有效策略。然而，现有方法通常通过晚期融合整合提示，缺乏在层次化特征表征上进行提示驱动的通道级调制的显式机制，无法充分捕捉深层上下文和模态特异变化。这限制了模型在医学图像分割中对语义提示的利用效率，是该论文要解决的核心问题。

Method: PCCA调制机制包含三个核心步骤：(1) 通过池化从特征图提取紧凑的通道描述符；(2) 将这些描述符投影到共享嵌入空间，实现提示信息与视觉特征的对齐；(3) 通过门控激励机制融合，计算提示感知的通道注意力权重。这些权重在编码器-解码器网络的多阶段自适应地重校准特征响应，实现跨层级的提示条件化语义增强。在此机制上构建PROMISE-Net框架，包含卷积架构的PROMISE-CNN（基于U-Net）和Transformer架构的PROMISE-Txformer（基于UNETR）两种变体，验证了PCCA作为通用调制模块的可扩展性与架构无关性。核心创新在于将提示从"晚期融合"转变为"深度层次化通道调制"，使提示信息能贯穿整个特征提取过程。

Result: 在四个医学图像分割基准上进行了系统评估：ISIC-Lesion(皮肤病变)、Kvasir-Polyp(息肉)、CAMUS-Cardiac(心脏超声)和Kvasir-Instrument(手术器械)。将PCCA集成到PROMISE-CNN后，相比基线U-Net，IoU分别提升10.4%、8.7%、0.8%和3.4%；PROMISE-Txformer相比基线UNETR，IoU分别提升7.6%、23.0%、2.1%和1.1%。结果表明PCCA(PROMISE-Net)在多种架构、成像模态和解剖目标上带来一致的性能改善，其中在息肉分割和基于Transformer的架构上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出了PCCA这一提示条件通道注意力机制，将交互式分割的语义提示从传统的晚期融合升级为深度层次化的通道级调制，并在卷积与Transformer两种架构上验证了其通用性和可扩展性。跨四个数据集、多种成像模态和解剖目标的稳定提升证明了该框架的泛化能力。潜在局限性包括：论文未详细讨论不同提示类型(点击vs框选vs涂鸦)对性能的影响、注意力权重的可解释性分析有限，以及提示信息的编码方式(单提示vs多提示)对模型的影响未充分探讨。

Abstract: Anatomically plausible segmentation remains challenging because of low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and modality-specific artifacts. Interactive segmentation has emerged as a promising strategy to guide feature extraction and improve localization, particularly in structurally ambiguous regions. However, existing methods integrate prompts through late-stage fusion and lack explicit mechanisms for prompt-driven channel-wise modulation across hierarchical feature representations, limiting their ability to capture deeper contextual and modality-specific variations. To address these limitations, we introduce Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA), a novel modulation mechanism that enables deep, hierarchical integration of semantic prompts within encoder-decoder networks. PCCA extracts compact channel descriptors via pooling, projects them into a shared space, and fuses them through a gated excitation mechanism to compute prompt-aware channel attention weights. These weights adaptively recalibrate feature responses across multiple network stages, enabling prompt-conditioned, semantically enriched hierarchical representations. Building on this, we propose PROMISE-Net, instantiated in two network variants: a convolutional model (PROMISE-CNN) and a transformer-based model (PROMISE-Txformer). Across the ISIC-Lesion, Kvasir-Polyp, CAMUS-Cardiac, and Kvasir-Instrument benchmarks, integrating PCCA into PROMISE-CNN yielded relative IoU gains of 10.4%, 8.7%, 0.8%, and 3.4%, respectively, over the baseline U-Net, while PROMISE-Txformer achieved corresponding gains of 7.6%, 23.0%, 2.1%, and 1.1%, respectively, over the baseline UNETR. These results show consistent improvements across architectures, imaging modalities, and anatomical targets, establishing PCCA and PROMISE-Net as a scalable, generalizable framework for prompt-aware hierarchical feature modulation in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [60] [Ultra-High-Definition Restoration Transformers with Correlation Matching Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20263)
*Cong Wang,Liyan Wang,Jinshan Pan,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren,Jun Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UHDformer++，一个通用的基于Transformer的UHD图像复原框架，在四个协同学习空间中联合建模：高分辨率空间(HR)提取多尺度特征、低分辨率空间(LR)学习紧凑特征、超分辨率空间(SR)上采样LR特征、以及低-高融合重建空间(LHFR)完成最终复原。通过FR-CMT模块与ACM模块桥接高低分辨率空间，该方法相比近期SOTA方法参数减少至少86%，并在低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨和去雪五个UHD复原任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHD图像（如4K/8K）复原任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪等）面临巨大挑战：UHD图像分辨率极高，直接在原始分辨率上运行基于Transformer的复原模型，其全局注意力计算复杂度随序列长度二次增长，导致参数量和计算成本难以承受。现有的UHD复原方法要么性能不足，要么模型过于臃肿难以实用。因此，需要一种既能高效处理高分辨率信息、又保持模型紧凑性的通用框架。UHDformer++针对这一问题，通过多分辨率协同学习空间的设计，在仅使用不到SOTA方法14%参数量（至少减少86%）的前提下，仍能获得更强的复原效果，这对于4K/8K视频处理、高清监控与专业影像应用具有重要实践意义。

Method: UHDformer++构建了一个四空间协同的Transformer框架：

1) **HR空间（高分辨率空间）**：提取多尺度高分辨率特征，并将它们与LR线索融合以生成残差图像，保留图像细节。

2) **LR空间（低分辨率空间）**：学习紧凑且具有代表性的特征。LR空间中的Transformer利用从HR空间蒸馏的互补表示来提升复原质量，这一设计大幅降低了主要计算开销。

3) **SR空间（超分辨率空间）**：在送入LHFR融合前，对LR输出进行超分辨率处理，为最终重建提供更丰富的特征。

4) **LHFR空间（低-高融合与重建）**：融合来自各空间的信息，执行最终图像复原。

两个关键桥接模块：
- **FR-CMT（Feature-Refined Correlation Matching Transformation）**：将HR特征的max-pooling与mean-pooling结果进行融合，从中选出top C/r个最具信息量的通道（C为通道数，r≥1控制压缩程度），用于替换LR Transformer中信息量较低的通道。这实现了一种跨分辨率的相关性匹配与特征精炼。
- **ACM（Adaptive Channel Modulator）**：自适应地重新校准多尺度HR特征，确保只有任务相关的信息传播到LR空间，抑制无关干扰。

Result: 该论文在5个UHD复原任务上进行了广泛实验：低光图像增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨和去雪。核心亮点是效率与性能的兼顾：相比近期SOTA方法，模型参数至少减少86%，同时取得显著的性能提升。论文声称在多个复原基准上均超越以往方法，但摘要未给出具体数据集名称（如UHD-LOL、UHD-Haze、UHD-Blur等）和详细指标数值。代码已开源（github.com/supersupercong/uhdformerplus），便于复现与后续研究。

Conclusion: UHDformer++的贡献在于：1) 提出一种通用的多子空间协同Transformer框架，以极高的参数效率统一解决多种UHD复原任务；2) 设计了FR-CMT和ACM两个创新模块，有效实现跨分辨率信息的精炼、选择与融合；3) 通过LR空间承载主要Transformer计算，SR空间恢复细节，实现了"低分辨率算力表达+高分辨率细节维护"的架构范式。该方法展示了多分辨率协同学习在超高清图像复原中的巨大潜力。局限性方面：摘要未提供各任务上的具体量化指标对比、运行效率（FLOPs/推理延迟）数据，以及该方法在任意超高清输入（如不同UHD分辨率）上的泛化能力说明；此外，统一的框架在不同复原任务上的适配灵活性也需进一步验证。

Abstract: We propose UHDformer++, a general Transformer-based framework to solve numerous Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) image restoration tasks. UHDformer++ operates across $4$ coordinated learning spaces: 1) a high-resolution space (HR) for multi-level feature extraction, 2) a low-resolution space (LR) for learning compact, representative features, 3) a super-resolution space (SR) for upsampling low-resolution features from SR, and 4) a low-high fusion and reconstruction space (LHFR) for final image restoration. Specifically, HR extracts multi-scale high-resolution features and fuses them with low-resolution cues to produce residual images, while LR distills complementary representations from HR to improve restoration quality. To supply LHFR with richer features, SR super-resolves LR outputs before fusion. We further introduce two modules to bridge the high- and low-resolution spaces. The Feature-Refined Correlation Matching Transformation (FR-CMT) module selects the top $C/r~(C~\text{denotes the number of channels;~}r\geq1~\text{controls the squeezing level})$, from the fusion between max- and mean-pooled high-resolution features to replace less informative channels in the low-resolution Transformer. The Adaptive Channel Modulator (ACM) adaptively recalibrates multi-scale high-resolution features, ensuring that only task-relevant information propagates to LR. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UHDformer++ reduces model parameters by at least 86\% compared with recent state-of-the-art methods while achieving substantial performance gains across $5$ UHD restoration tasks, including low-light image enhancement, dehazing, deblurring, deraining, and desnowing. Code will be released at https://github.com/supersupercong/uhdformerplus.

</details>


### [61] [CalcSeg: Confidence-aware 3D Latent Context Curriculum Learning For Myocardial Scar Segmentation From Single-Stack LGE-CMRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20305)
*Nivetha Jayakumar,Hannah Kim,Amit R. Patel,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CalcSeg，一个基于置信度感知的潜在上下文课程学习框架，用于单栈LGE-CMR图像的心肌瘢痕分割。其核心创新包括：(1)动态半监督课程学习策略，通过融合预测误差、认知不确定性和瘢痕负荷估计的置信度评分函数来自动评估样本难度并渐进式训练；(2)潜在切片级自注意力机制，从稀疏的2D切片中捕获层间依赖，推断3D空间表征以弥补单栈采集空间上下文有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌瘢痕分割是临床上的长期痛点，尤其在LGE-CMR单栈成像场景中面临多重挑战：组织对比度低、瘢痕区域弥散且尺寸小，同时单栈采集严重限制了可用的3D空间上下文。这些特性使得监督学习方法难以有效训练，尤其在需要大量标注且样本难度分布不均的情况下。现有方法未能充分利用有限的3D结构信息，也未针对样本难度差异设计自适应的训练策略。

Method: CalcSeg包含两大核心技术模块。(1)动态半监督课程学习：提出一个置信度感知的评分函数，将预测瘢痕图的误差、认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）量化以及瘢痕负荷估计三者整合，无需人工标注即可自动评估样本难度，从而渐进式地从简单案例扩展到更具挑战性的瘢痕案例进行训练。(2)潜在切片级自注意力（latent slice-wise self-attention）：直接从稀疏的单栈2D输入中捕获层间依赖关系，推断并融合3D特征表征，以补偿单栈采集固有的空间上下文不足问题。

Result: 方法在多个中心的临床LGE-CMR数据集上进行了评估，并与现有瘢痕分割网络进行了基准对比。实验结果表明CalcSeg在所有对比方法中一致取得最优性能，尤其在临床上具有挑战性的案例（如低对比度、弥散性和小区域瘢痕）上表现出显著提升。代码已在Github上开源。

Conclusion: CalcSeg的主要贡献在于：(1)针对挑战性医疗图像分割提出了一种置信度感知的动态课程学习范式，通过结合不确定性与样本难度估计实现无需标注的自适应训练；(2)设计了从稀疏2D输入推断3D上下文的潜在自注意力机制，为空间上下文受限的成像模态提供了有效的特征增强方案。局限在于其有效性主要验证于LGE-CMR单栈场景，其课程学习与3D推断机制在其他模态和数据集上的泛化能力有待进一步探索。

Abstract: Myocardial scar segmentation from single-stack late gadolinium-enhanced cardiac magnetic resonance (LGE-CMR) imaging has been a longstanding and clinically important challenge, particularly in the presence of low tissue contrast, diffuse, and small scar regions. These challenges are further intensified by the limited availability of 3D spatial context. This paper presents CalcSeg, a Confidence-aware latent context curriculum learning framework that leverages fused 3D feature representations from single-stack 2D LGE-CMR images for robust scar segmentation. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic semi-supervised curriculum learning strategy that progressively expands training from easier to more challenging scar cases using a learned confidence-aware scoring function. Such a function integrates errors in the predicted scar maps with quantified epistemic uncertainty and scar burden estimation to automatically assess sample difficulty without requiring manual labels. To compensate for the limited spatial context in single-stack acquisitions, we then develop a latent slice-wise self-attention to capture inter-slice dependencies and infer 3D spatial representations from sparse 2D inputs. We evaluate CalcSeg on multi-center clinical LGE-CMR datasets and benchmark against existing scar segmentation networks. Experimental results show that CalcSeg consistently outperforms all competing methods, particularly with substantial improvements on clinically challenging cases. Our code is released on Github.

</details>


### [62] [DreamHand: Repurposing Video Diffusion Models for Occlusion-Robust Egocentric 3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20308)
*Yufei Liu,Xixi Wang,Hao Li,Ganlong Zhao,Kaitong Cai,Chengkai Jin,Chunxiao Liu,Jianbo Liu,Siyuan Huang,Xingang Pan,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamHand 是一种离线（clip-level）的 3D 手部轨迹恢复框架，核心创新在于将视频扩散模型（VDM）改造成确定性几何编码器：在单次前向传播中利用干净潜变量（clean latent）揭示当前观测之外的手部信息（包括被遮挡和出画手部），配合双向时空解码器恢复连续的双手度量轨迹。在五个第一人称基准上达到 SOTA，在 ARCTIC 和 HOT3D 上分别将 MPJPE-p 降低 30% 和 40%；当评估纳入出画手部时，增益达 46%–61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称（egocentric）视频为具身智能提供了可扩展的操作数据，但从中恢复度量级 3D 手部轨迹面临严重挑战：物体遮挡频繁、手部经常短暂离开画面。现有方法存在明显局限——单帧回归器和窗口化时间回归器在手部出画时立即失效；而基于视频扩散模型（VDM）的方法仅把扩散模型当像素级渲染器使用，依赖重负载的随机多步采样，难以用于轨迹恢复。核心动机是：需要一个能超越当前观测、在遮挡和出画情况下仍能推断手部几何结构的高效解决方案。

Method: DreamHand 将 VDM 重新定位为确定性几何编码器（Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder）：对干净潜变量做单次前向传播，即可暴露遮挡及出画手部的内容，从而将原本随机、多步采样的扩散模型转化为一次前向的确定性特征提取器。整个框架为离线 clip 级架构，包含两个核心组件：① 确定性干净潜变量编码器提取时空特征；② 双向时空解码器（Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder）解码连续双手轨迹。该方法无需外部检测器即可恢复连续双手度量轨迹。此外，论文提出 Ray-Based Camera Solver，支持无需测试时相机内参的第二种配置，增强了实际部署的灵活性。

Result: 在五个第一人称（egocentric）基准数据集上评测，DreamHand 均达到新的 SOTA。关键结果：① 在遮挡严重的 ARCTIC 上，MPJPE-p（对齐后的平均关节位置误差）降低 30%；② 在 HOT3D 上降低 40%；③ 当评估中纳入出画手部时，增益提升至 46%–61%。这表明模型在遮挡和出画等挑战场景下的鲁棒性增益远大于常规可见场景。方法在无外部检测器条件下实现了度量级双手轨迹恢复，并额外支持无需测试时相机内参的配置。

Conclusion: DreamHand 的主要贡献在于范式转变：不再把视频扩散模型当作像素级渲染器，而是将其重建为一个单次前向的确定性几何编码器，从而在无需重采样的情况下利用扩散模型强大的先验能力还原被遮挡与出画手部的几何信息。双向时空解码器与 Ray-Based Camera Solver 分别解决了时序连续性约束和无内参部署问题。该方法为从日常人类视频扩展到机器人操作数据提供了可扩展路径。潜在局限包括：属于离线（clip-level）框架而非在线实时系统，硬件开销与推理延迟可能限制其在实时机器人和无人机感知场景中的直接部署。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers scalable manipulation data for embodied AI, yet recovering metric 3D hand trajectories remains challenging due to severe object occlusion and frequent out-of-sight gaps. Existing single-frame and windowed temporal regressors fail when hand shortly leaves the frame, while recent video diffusion models (VDMs) rely on heavy, stochastic multi-step sampling as pixel-space renderers. We instead repurpose VDM into a deterministic geometry encoder. A single forward pass over the clean latent exposes scene content beyond current observations, including occluded and out-of-sight hands. We introduce DreamHand, an offline clip-level framework that extracts features via a Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder and decodes them with a Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder. DreamHand recovers continuous bimanual trajectories with metric placement and no external detector, while a Ray-Based Camera Solver supports a second configuration that needs no test-time camera intrinsics. Across five egocentric benchmarks, DreamHand sets a new state of the art, cutting MPJPE-p by 30% on occlusion-heavy ARCTIC and 40% on HOT3D. These gains reach 46%-61% once out-of-sight hands are included in the evaluation, offering a scalable path from everyday human video to robot manipulation data.

</details>


### [63] [Inter-X++: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Human-Human Interaction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20312)
*Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Zili Lin,Xintao Lv,Yichao Yan,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Inter-X++，一个大规模、高保真的人-人交互（HHI）多模态基准数据集，包含 11,388 段交互序列、超过 810 万帧，由新颖的混合动捕系统采集，覆盖全身动作与精细手指关节运动。同时提出 OpenHHI，一个统一的 HHI 表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建与语义理解，在生成与感知任务上均达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HHI 数据集与建模方法存在三重根本性瓶颈：(1) 运动学保真度低，缺少灵巧手部手势的精细建模；(2) 缺乏丰富的多模态标注，限制了交互的语义理解；(3) 交互表示碎片化且评估协议不一致，阻碍了公平严格的基准评测。这些瓶颈导致生成（交互合成）与感知（交互理解）两大范式长期割裂，难以建立统一的 HHI 表示。系统地解决这些问题对构建智能数字人系统至关重要。

Method: 该工作包含两大核心贡献：(1) 数据集层面——采用新颖的混合动捕系统采集高保真全身运动及手指关节细节，并为其配备多层面标注：层级化细粒度文本描述、交互类别、因果交互顺序、对象间关系与个性、顶点级接触图以及物理正则化约束。(2) 方法层面——提出 OpenHHI，一个单一且统一的 HHI 表示与建模框架，将交互重建与语义理解联合优化于同一表示空间，从而桥接交互理解与生成。数据集构建了四类对称覆盖生成与感知范式的下游任务，并系统标准化了交互表示与评测协议以消除评测歧义。

Result: Inter-X++ 提供 11,388 段高保真交互序列、超 8.1M 帧，涵盖精确的全身动作与细粒度手指关节运动，并配有层级化文本、交互类别、因果顺序、关系/个性、接触图等丰富标注。基于此基准，OpenHHI 在生成与感知任务上均取得 SOTA 性能，实验证明统一表示能够同时桥接交互理解与生成。论文通过系统化的评测协议标准化，确保了对比的公平性与严格性。

Conclusion: Inter-X++ 是首个兼顾运动学保真度、灵巧手部细节与多模态语义标注的大规模 HHI 基准；OpenHHI 验证了统一表示可以打通交互理解与生成两个范式。主要局限可能在于模型开销与推理效率问题（生成+感知联合优化可能带来计算代价），以及基准评测对特定动捕系统的依赖性和跨域泛化能力有待进一步验证。

Abstract: The capability to perceive and synthesize human-human interactions is fundamental to developing intelligent digital human systems. However, existing datasets and modeling approaches are fundamentally constrained by low-fidelity kinematics, the omission of dexterous hand gestures and a severe lack of rich multimodal annotations. Furthermore, fragmented interaction representations and inconsistent evaluation protocols also impede fair and rigorous benchmarking. To systematically address these bottlenecks, we present Inter-X++, a comprehensive and large-scale benchmark designed to empower versatile HHI analysis. Captured via a novel hybrid motion capture system, Inter-X++ provides 11,388 high-fidelity interaction sequences and over 8.1M frames, featuring precise whole-body movements and detailed finger articulations. Meanwhile, we enrich the data foundation with multifaceted annotations, including hierarchical fine-grained textual descriptions, interaction categories, causal interaction orders, the relationship and personality of the subjects, as well as vertex-level contact maps and physically regularized constraints. Leveraging these elaborate annotations, we formulate a unified testing ground comprising four categories of downstream tasks that symmetrically span both generative and perceptive paradigms. To eliminate benchmarking ambiguities, we systematically standardize the interaction representations and evaluation protocols. Finally, we go beyond dataset construction to propose OpenHHI, a single and unified HHI representation and modeling framework that jointly optimizes interaction reconstruction and semantic understanding. Extensive experiments reveal that OpenHHI achieves state-of-the-art performance on both generation and perception tasks. This definitively proves that our unified representation successfully bridges interaction understanding and generation simultaneously.

</details>


### [64] [Swift-Image: Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334)
*Taihang Hu,Zhao Wang,Zuan Gao,Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Zhengze Xu,Yuhang Yu,Yongchao Du,Xingjian Wang,Jun Zheng,Qinye Zhou,Zhengrui Chen,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Zhengtao Wu,Ge Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Denghui Yang,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Swift-Image 是一个紧凑的 6B 参数统一模型，同时支持文生图、单图编辑和多图编辑任务。通过系统化训练工程（渐进式训练管线、并行专家强化学习、多教师在线蒸馏、Prompt 增强器、结构剪枝与少步蒸馏），在 243K GPU 训练小时和 6B 参数的约束下达到开源模型中领先的整体性能，并产出近乎无损的 3B 压缩版和加速推理变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的核心动机是探索在有限计算预算下，一个相对较小的视觉生成器能通过系统性训练工程被推进多远。现有生成模型往往依赖大规模参数和巨大训练算力，而 Swift-Image 试图证明：即使只有 6B 参数（单流 DiT 架构），配合精心设计的训练管线，也能在文生图、单图编辑、多图编辑等多个异构任务上达到领先水平。该问题的重要性在于降低大规模视觉生成模型的训练与部署门槛，使高质量多能力生成模型更可负担、更易落地。

Method: Swift-Image 的技术方案包含多方面创新：(1) 架构上采用高效的 6B 单流 DiT（diffusion transformer），作为统一生成主干；(2) 渐进式训练管线，从宽泛语义覆盖逐步演进到更高分辨率、更强视觉质量和统一的生成-编辑联合监督，形成数据课程；(3) 后训练阶段采用并行专家强化学习（parallel expert RL），随后用多教师在线蒸馏（multi-teacher on-policy distillation）缓解异构目标之间的干扰；(4) 将高层推理与像素级渲染解耦，引入 Prompt Enhancer 把用户请求翻译成与生成器对齐的视觉规格描述；(5) 面向高效部署，采用结构剪枝和少步蒸馏，产出 3B 参数和加速推理的变体。

Result: 论文在多个生成与编辑任务的聚合指标上评估，Swift-Image 以仅 6B 参数和 243K GPU 训练小时在已评测的开源模型中达到领先的聚合性能。压缩后的 3B 模型性能几乎无损；少步蒸馏在显著减少采样步数的同时进一步提升了聚合编辑性能。上述结果验证了训练工程（数据课程、后训练策略、提示增强、模型压缩）对挖掘小模型潜力具有决定性作用。

Conclusion: Swift-Image 的主要贡献在于系统性地展示了训练工程对小型统一生成模型能力的挖掘，提出了一套可复用的方法论：渐进式数据课程、并行专家 RL 与多教师蒸馏、Prompt 增强、以及剪枝+少步蒸馏的组合压缩策略，并验证了 6B 最小规模下超越更大开源模型的可能性。其局限性在于：报告主要面向开源模型聚合指标，未对单一任务的极限性能做深入细化；具体的数据配比、RL 专家分工与蒸馏细节有待进一步公开；对可控性、生成安全性等维度未作深入讨论。就事件相机/机器人感知等论题，本文关联不大。

Abstract: We present Swift-Image, a compact unified model for text-to-image generation, single-image editing, and multi-image editing. Our goal is to explore how far a relatively small visual generator can be pushed through systematic training engineering under a constrained computational budget. Swift-Image adopts an efficient 6B single-stream DiT and a progressive training pipeline that evolves from broad semantic coverage to higher resolution, stronger visual quality, and unified generation-editing supervision. For post-training, we employ parallel expert reinforcement learning followed by multi-teacher on-policy distillation to alleviate interference among heterogeneous objectives. We further decouple high-level reasoning from pixel-level rendering with a Prompt Enhancer that translates user requests into generator-aligned visual specifications. For efficient deployment, structural pruning and few-step distillation produce 3B and accelerated variants. Swift-Image achieves leading aggregate performance among evaluated open-source models with only 6B parameters and 243K GPU training hours; the compressed 3B model incurs nearly no loss, while few-step distillation further improves aggregate editing performance with substantially fewer sampling steps. Our study also summarizes practical lessons for architecture, data curriculum, post-training, prompt enhancement, and model compression.

</details>


### [65] [4DAnyone: Create Anyone in 4D from a Casual Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20335)
*Yudong Jin,Tao Xie,Qihang Zhang,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DAnyone提出了一种从无标定的单目视频重建4D人体的框架，其核心思路是先用视频扩散模型生成具有重建级多视角一致性的新视角视频，再将其提升为4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）。针对目标视角数量超出单次DiT前向传播容量、导致多视角不一致的问题，论文将其识别为"有界注意力上下文"问题，并提出两个互补设计：参考上下文打包（RCP）将不断增长的参考视角压缩为固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，将参考上下文复杂度降为O(1)；目标上下文路由（TCR）在去噪过程中轮转目标视角分组，实现跨组上下文共享和细节稳定。在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans数据集上，该方法在新视角视频质量和下游4DGS重建两方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相机控制视频扩散模型虽然能合成较为合理的新视角视频，但当目标视角数量扩大到4DGS重建所需的数十个视角时，这些模型无法保持跨视角一致性。论文将这一失败根源剖析为"有界注意力上下文"问题：当目标视角超过单次DiT前向传播的容量限制时，必须将视角拆分到多个分组中处理，这暴露了两个耦合瓶颈——(1) 参考上下文侧：对之前生成的所有视角进行条件化会随生成视角数线性增长（O(N)），削弱了跨视角的外观引导；(2) 目标上下文侧：不相交的分组无法直接交换信息，导致全局结构漂移。这一问题直接制约了"视频扩散→4DGS提升"这一实用重建管线的可行性，对于4D人体重建、虚拟内容生成等应用具有重要意义。

Method: 4DAnyone包含两个核心技术设计以同时解决两个耦合瓶颈：1) 参考上下文打包（Reference Context Packing, RCP）：随着生成视角增加，参考上下文规模呈O(N)增长，RCP将不断增长的参考视角压缩为一个固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，将参考上下文复杂度降为O(1)，在不牺牲外观信息的前提下维持对生成网络的稳定引导。2) 目标上下文路由（Target Context Routing, TCR）：在去噪过程中动态轮转目标视角的分组方式——在高噪声步通过旋转分组共享跨组上下文以确保全局结构一致，在低噪声步则稳定局部细节。此外，论文构建了MVGameHuman数据集（基于内部游戏引擎生成），并与light-stage数据集及in-the-wild视频数据集联合训练。整体管线为：从无标定单目视频出发，先由改进的视频扩散模型生成多视角一致的视频，再将其提升为4DGS进行4D人体重建。

Result: 论文在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans两个基准数据集上进行了实验评估。评估内容涵盖两个层面：新视角视频质量（novel-view video quality）和下游4DGS重建质量。实验结果表明，4DAnyone在上述两项指标上均优于此前的方法，并展现出较强的in-the-wild（野外真实场景）泛化能力。论文提供了项目页面（https://4danyone.github.io）展示视频结果和源代码。尽管摘要未给出具体的定量指标数值，但明确指出其在视频质量和重建质量两方面均取得了领先性能。

Conclusion: 4DAnyone的核心贡献在于：第一，诊断出大规模多视角扩散中的"有界注意力上下文"问题，并将其细化为参考上下文与目标上下文两个耦合瓶颈；第二，提出RCP和TCR两个互补性设计，分别以O(1)复杂度压缩参考上下文、并在去噪过程中路由目标分组上下文，从而实现了从单目视频到重建级多视角一致视频再到4DGS的端到端管线；第三，构建了规模化的MVGameHuman数据集以补充训练数据。该方法在视频生成质量和下游4D重建上均超越现有方法，并具有良好的野外泛化能力。可能的局限性包括：依赖游戏引擎合成数据与light-stage数据的组合训练，对实际极端复杂户外场景的适应性仍需进一步验证，且摘要中未披露具体的量化指标与计算开销细节。

Abstract: We present 4DAnyone, a framework for reconstructing 4D humans from an uncalibrated monocular video by generating reconstruction-grade multiview-consistent videos and lifting them into 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS). Existing camera-controlled video diffusion models synthesize plausible novel-view videos but fail to maintain consistency when scaled to the tens of target views required for 4DGS reconstruction. We identify this failure as a bounded-attention-context problem: when target views exceed the capacity of a single DiT forward pass, they must be split into groups, exposing two coupled bottlenecks. On the reference-context side, conditioning on all previously generated views grows as $O(N)$, weakening cross-view appearance guidance. On the target-context side, disjoint groups cannot directly exchange information, causing global structural drift. 4DAnyone addresses both bottlenecks with two complementary designs: Reference Context Packing (RCP) compresses growing reference views into a fixed-length mixed-resolution context with $O(1)$ reference-context complexity, while Target Context Routing (TCR) rotates target-view groupings during denoising to share context across groups at high-noise steps and stabilize details at low-noise steps. We further build the MVGameHuman dataset using our in-house game engine and combine it with light-stage and in-the-wild video datasets for training. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and DyMVHumans show that 4DAnyone outperforms prior methods in both novel-view video quality and downstream 4DGS reconstruction, with robust in-the-wild generalization. See our project page for video results and source code: https://4danyone.github.io.

</details>


### [66] [WithEveryone: Unified Planning and Identity Grounding for Group Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20336)
*Hengyuan Xu,Qixun Wang,Yiji Cheng,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Wei Cheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向最多10个参考身份的群像生成统一框架WithEveryone。其核心创新在于：将每个身份注入为可寻址token，预测结构化“身份-布局”计划并将其渲染为视觉条件，同时提出Layout-Grounded ID Loss，利用标注的人脸区域直接监督目标身份，替代不稳定的嵌入基人脸匹配。在身份不相交的基准上，将目标上下文身份相似度从GPT-Image-2的0.462提升至0.499，将复制粘贴伪影由0.169降至0.055，身份覆盖率97.3%，重复率仅2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当一张场景图必须包含多个指定人物时，身份保持的图像生成会急剧退化。除了要保留每个身份外，模型还必须将每个参考身份绑定到图中特定且不同的人与位置，而训练时的身份损失需要在多个带噪声的预测人脸间建立对应关系。这带来三个核心难题：身份数量增多导致歧义激增、身份-位置绑定缺乏显式监督、嵌入基人脸匹配在噪声人脸预测下不稳定。该问题直接关系文本到图像生成的可控性与身份保真度，是通用可控生成落地群像/合影类真实应用（如团队照片、社交群像）的关键。

Method: WithEveryone由三阶段构成：1) 身份注入：将每个选定身份编码为“可寻址token”（addressed token），使其在生成过程中可被定位；2) 结构化身份-布局计划：模型先预测一个将身份与人脸框位置绑定的结构化计划，并将该计划渲染为视觉条件（visual condition）参与图像合成；3) 两个关键训练目标——(a) Layout-Grounded ID Loss：利用标注的人脸区域（face regions）直接监督期望身份，避免不稳定的嵌入基人脸匹配，从而建立参考身份与图中位置之间的显式对应；(b) ID Representation Forcing：在图像合成之前的生成阶段，为每个身份预先训练一个预测分支。整体框架将显式的身份-布局接地（identity-layout grounding）作为缩放群像生成的关键机制，而非依赖对参考人脸的直接复制。

Result: 在身份不相交（identity-disjoint）的基准上进行评估，确保训练与测试身份不重叠。评价指标包括目标上下文身份相似度（target-context identity similarity）、复制粘贴伪影比率（copy-paste artifacts）以及身份覆盖与重复率。主要结果：1) 目标上下文身份相似度从GPT-Image-2的0.462提升到0.499；2) 复制粘贴伪影从0.169显著降至0.055（约为原来的1/3）；3) 覆盖97.3%的请求身份，重复率仅2.8%。结果表明显式布局接地与平面有监督损失能有效抑制复制粘贴伪影并提升多身份保真度。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：首次提出面向多达十个参考身份的统一框架WithEveryone，通过“可寻址token + 结构化身份-布局计划 + 视觉条件渲染”实现身份-布局的显式绑定；并首创Layout-Grounded ID Loss，用布局标注直接监督身份映射，规避了嵌入基脸匹配的不稳定性；ID Representation Forcing进一步在合成前强化逐身份预测。实验证明该方法在身份相似度、复制粘贴伪影抑制及身份覆盖率上全面超越GPT-Image-2。局限性：依赖于人脸区域标注，需要显式的身份-布局监督数据；评估聚焦于相关指标，未充分探讨大背景多样性、属性混淆或极端遮挡下的人群场景；对参考人脸直接复制之外的真实身份差异与姿态/光照变化仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Identity-preserving image generation becomes increasingly unreliable when a scene must contain many specified people. Beyond retaining each identity, the model must bind every reference to a distinct person and location, while training-time identity losses must establish correspondence among several noisy predicted faces. We introduce WithEveryone, a unified framework for generating group images up to ten reference identities. WithEveryone injects each selected identity as an addressed token, predicts a structured identity--layout plan, and renders the plan as a visual condition. Its key objective, Layout-Grounded ID Loss, uses annotated face regions to supervise the intended identities directly, avoiding unstable embedding-based face matching; ID Representation Forcing additionally trains a prediction for each identity before image synthesis. On an identity-disjoint benchmark, WithEveryone achieves the highest target-context identity similarity, improving face similarity from 0.462 for GPT-Image-2 to 0.499, while reducing copy-paste artifacts from 0.169 to 0.055. It further covers 97.3\% of the requested identities with a duplicate rate of only 2.8\%. These results show that explicit identity--layout grounding enables identity-preserving generation to scale to larger groups without relying on direct reference-face copying.

</details>

### [67] [Active Spiking Perception: The Membrane Potential as a Belief State for Anytime 3D Point Cloud Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19232)
*Akarsh Jain,Arya Pawa,Ayush Debnath,Smera Rawal,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出Active Spiking Perception (ASP)，将3D点云识别重新定义为基于脉冲网络自身膜电位的迭代主动决策过程。网络利用LIF膜电位作为类别运行信念（running belief），动态选择下一个观测点云分块，并基于置信度裕度实现提前退出。该机制以约2%的额外骨干参数实现端到端训练，推理时退化为argmax，并在ModelNet40/10、ShapeNetPart及S3DIS Area 5上取得具有竞争力的性能，且首个在S3DIS Area 5上报告脉冲网络结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲点云网络通常以固定的、与输入无关的顺序扫描空间，这浪费了脉冲计算最独特的资源——膜电位的时序演化，未将其用作决策的依据。作者认为，膜电位的动态演化本可作为一种"自适应感知"的载体：网络应当能够根据当前已看到的信息自主决定下一步观察哪里、何时停止，从而更高效地利用计算资源。这一问题的意义在于：主动感知（active perception）能够显著降低计算/能量开销，同时保持识别精度，对边缘实时部署尤为关键。

Method: ASP将3D识别重构为迭代决策过程：(1) 通过LIF膜电位读取类别运行信念；(2) 轻量Slice-Selection Policy基于膜状态和预计算几何描述符，为未访问的farthest-point-sampled（FPS）分块打分；(3) 通过straight-through Gumbel-Softmax实现端到端训练，推理时退化为argmax，仅增加约2%骨干参数。基于置信度裕度的提前退出规则触发早期终止。作者给出三项理论证明：漏电积分等价于贝叶斯滤波器的递归log-posterior更新；退出规则在停止时刻实现无分布选择风险且无多重检验惩罚；流式状态前向传递与前缀重计算精确等价（仅含有界有限精度漂移）。机制可无缝迁移到稠密预测（分块选择）及注视式非脉冲Transformer（以fixation替代chunk selection），证明策略与脉冲骨干解耦。计算成本与观测次数严格线性相关，阈值成为能量调节旋钮（较基线节能2.8x至1.35x倍）。

Result: 实验在多个基准上验证：ModelNet40达90.62%、ModelNet10达93.28%，相比更大骨干的最强脉冲基线低1.7个点，但提供了任何基线都不具备的"经认证的任意时刻接口"（certified anytime interface）。稠密预测方面，ShapeNetPart达到83.21实例mIoU，S3DIS Area 5达到48.50 mIoU——据作者所述为首个在S3DIS Area 5上的脉冲结果。能量与计算开销随观测次数线性增长，阈值可灵活调节，节能范围从2.8x到1.35x。局限：在所用裁剪尺寸下S3DIS有一个类别无法识别，作者给出了对应的修复方案（预测）。

Conclusion: ASP的核心贡献在于将膜电位从单纯的中间计算状态提升为决策的中心媒介，实现了脉冲网络中的主动分块选择与置信度驱动的提前退出，并赋予其严格的理论保证（贝叶斯一致性、无分布选择风险、流式精确性）。其策略与骨干解耦的设计使其能同时应用于脉冲和非脉冲（Transformer）骨干，具备广泛的适用性。局限包括在特定数据集上精度仍略低于最强脉冲基线，以及S3DIS单个类别在裁剪尺寸下的不可识别问题。整体而言，ASP为3D感知提供了一条面向实时、节能、带终止保证的任意时刻推理新路径。

Abstract: Spiking point cloud networks usually scan space in a fixed, input-agnostic order, which leaves the most distinctive resource of spiking computation, the temporal evolution of the membrane potential, unused as a locus of decision-making. Active Spiking Perception (ASP) recasts 3D recognition as an iterative decision process in which the network's own leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) membrane potential, read as a running belief over the class, selects the next chunk to observe and triggers confidence-margin early exit. A lightweight Slice-Selection Policy scores unvisited farthest-point-sampled chunks from the membrane state and precomputed geometric descriptors, trains end-to-end through a straight-through Gumbel-Softmax, reduces to an argmax at inference, and adds about 2% of backbone parameters. We prove that leaky integration is the recursive log-posterior update of a Bayesian filter, that the exit rule attains distribution-free selective risk with no multiple-testing penalty at the stopping time, and that streaming state carry-forward is exactly equivalent to prefix recomputation with bounded finite-precision drift. ASP reaches 90.62% and 93.28% on ModelNet40 and ModelNet10, 1.7 points below the strongest spiking baseline at a larger backbone, while adding a certified anytime interface no baseline offers. The mechanism transfers unchanged to dense prediction, giving 83.21 instance mIoU on ShapeNetPart and 48.50 mIoU on S3DIS Area 5, to our knowledge the first spiking results on S3DIS Area 5, and, fixation replacing chunk selection, to a foveated non-spiking transformer, so the policy is not tied to spiking backbones: cost is exactly linear in observations and the threshold is a measured compute dial spanning 2.8x to 1.35x less energy. One limitation is concrete: one S3DIS class is unidentifiable at the crop size we use, and we give the prediction that would fix it.

</details>


### [68] [Spiking Local Interaction and Adaptive Complementary Fusion for Spiking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19238)
*Dongcheng Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Zhenyu Yang,Zhiyuan Li,Jinyan Yu,Yongjian Wang,Tiechui Yao,Wenli Zhang,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文针对Spiking Transformer中脉冲自注意力（SSA）的二值化查询/键表示会抑制弱关系、限制局部空间上下文传播的问题，提出了两类轻量级改进：Spiking Local Interaction（SLI）——通过深度可分离卷积建立独立于注意力的邻居token直接信息交换通路；以及Adaptive Complementary Fusion（ACF）——通过逐层、逐通道的可学习系数将SSA与SLI自适应融合。该方法保留原始注意力形式、参数开销小，可嵌入多种Spiking Transformer架构，并在图像分类（ImageNet-1K、CIFAR）、事件识别（CIFAR10-DVS）和语义分割（ADE20K）中取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Spiking Transformer通过脉冲自注意力（SSA）建模token交互，但SSA将查询和键编码成二值脉冲表示，导致连续语义相似度被映射为稀疏、离散的关系响应，从而：(1) 抑制了弱相关token间的关系表达；(2) 限制了局部空间上下文信息的传播。由于事件相机和边缘感知场景常依赖局部纹理与空间相关性，这一局限对事件流识别和小目标理解尤为不利。因此需要在不破坏注意力机制原始形式的前提下，引入注意力无关的局部交互通路来补充SSA的信息瓶颈。

Method: 论文提出两种核心组件，可即插即用地嵌入任意Spiking Transformer：1) Spiking Local Interaction (SLI)——构建一条独立于SSA的路径，通过轻量级深度可分离卷积（depthwise-pointwise变换）实现相邻脉冲token之间的直接信息交换，在不依赖全局注意力匹配的情况下传播局部空间上下文；2) Adaptive Complementary Fusion (ACF)——针对不同网络深度，为每个通道学习一组可学习的融合系数，按层按通道自适应平衡SSA与SLI两种交互模式的贡献，取代固定的加权策略。整体设计保留原始注意力公式，仅引入适度参数开销，且不改变架构的脉冲驱动特性，因此可无缝嵌入QKFormer等不同Spiking Transformer骨干。

Result: 实验在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、CIFAR10-DVS（事件数据）和ADE20K上进行，覆盖图像分类、事件识别与语义分割三个任务。主要结果：QKFormer集成SLI与ACF后在ImageNet-1K上达到84.37% Top-1准确率；在ADE20K上达到37.5% mIoU，且该分割模型无需ImageNet预训练。消融研究证实SSA与SLI捕捉互补的交互模式，可学习的ACF融合系数显著优于固定权重融合。所有数据集上均获得一致改进，表明方法的通用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统分析了Spiking Transformer中SSA二值化表示导致的局部上下文信息瓶颈，并提出SLI+ACF这一即插即用的互补交互融合方案，在不改变注意力核心公式的前提下，以微小参数开销显著提升分类、事件识别和语义分割性能。其价值在于揭示并弥补了SNN注意力机制在局部空间建模上的缺陷，且方法架构无关、可迁移到多种Spiking Transformer。潜在局限包括：实验未覆盖更大的基座架构规模下的端到端分割预训练、事件流上的实时推理能耗优势尚未量化，以及深度可分离局部交互对极细粒度小目标是否足够有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers model token interactions primarily through spiking self-attention (SSA). However, binary query and key representations map continuous similarities to sparse and discrete relation responses, which may suppress weak relations and limit the propagation of local spatial context. To address this limitation, we introduce Spiking Local Interaction (SLI) and Adaptive Complementary Fusion (ACF). SLI establishes an attention-independent pathway for direct information exchange among neighboring spiking tokens using lightweight depthwise--pointwise transformations. ACF integrates SSA and SLI through layer-specific, channel-wise coefficients that adaptively balance their contributions at different network depths. The proposed design preserves the original attention formulation and can be incorporated into different Spiking Transformer architectures with modest parameter overhead. Experiments on ImageNet-1K, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, CIFAR10-DVS, and ADE20K show consistent improvements across image classification, event-based recognition, and semantic segmentation. In particular, QKFormer with SLI and ACF achieves $84.37\%$ Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K and $37.5\%$ mIoU on ADE20K, where the segmentation model is trained without ImageNet pretraining. Ablation studies and qualitative analyses further indicate that SSA and SLI capture complementary interaction patterns and that learnable fusion consistently outperforms fixed weighting.

</details>


### [69] [BASC : Behavior-Aligned Quantization and Pruning for Low-Bit Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19239)
*Linliang Chen,Yan Zhong,Xin Liu,Sai Li,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Behavior-Aligned SNN Compression (BASC)，一个统一的SNN压缩框架，通过两个轻量模块（TSC和BIC）分别解决量化和剪枝中的"准则-行为不匹配"问题：TSC在时间域损失下让缩放因子可学习以对齐尖峰时序，BIC利用通道间依赖仅在剪枝阈值附近重新评估通道重要性。在静态和神经形态基准上，更低比特的BASC模型能匹配或超越更高比特基线，且结构化剪枝后仍保持精度优势，同时进一步降低模型存储和突触运算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能SNN虽然具备事件驱动和能量高效的优势，但其存储和逐时间步计算开销巨大，难以部署在资源受限设备上。量化和剪枝是两种互补的压缩手段，但二者的决策准则都存在缺陷：量化使用局部准则，忽视了时间维度上的任务反馈（尖峰时序的变化）；剪枝则忽略通道间的依赖关系。这种"准则-行为不匹配"（criterion-behavior mismatch）导致即便各自优化局部准则，压缩结果仍非最优。因此需要一个统一的框架，将尖峰时序行为和通道间联合驱动关系纳入压缩决策。

Method: BASC 框架包含两个轻量模块：(1) 对量化，由于缩放因子（scale）施加于每个时间步的突触电流会改变尖峰时刻，作者提出 Temporal-Behavior Scale Correction (TSC)，在时间域损失下使缩放因子可学习，让尖峰行为反馈（firing behavior）直接指导缩放因子的优化；(2) 对剪枝，由于通道重要性取决于通道间如何联合驱动膜电位越过发放阈值，作者提出 Boundary-Level Inter-Channel Correction (BIC)，先用通道级重要性分数做初始选择，再仅对剪枝阈值附近的通道引入通道间信息进行重新评估，以兼顾效率与准确性。整体设计保持轻量化，不引入明显额外开销。

Result: 作者在静态基准和神经形态（neuromorphic）基准数据上进行了大量实验。结果显示：低比特位宽的 BASC 模型能匹配甚至超越更高比特位的基线模型；在进行结构化剪枝后，BASC 模型仍保持这一精度优势。同时 BASC 在保持精度的前提下进一步减少了模型存储空间和突触运算量（synaptic operations），验证了量化和剪枝两条路径结合的有效性及框架的通用性。

Conclusion: BASC 的核心贡献在于首次将"行为对齐"思想系统性地引入SNN压缩：量化侧通过时间域可学习scale纠正尖峰时序偏移，剪枝侧通过通道间依赖修正阈值边界处的选择错误，二者共享统一的准则-行为对齐原则。其意义在于为事件驱动SNN的落地部署提供了统一的、无需增加额外开销的压缩方案。可能的局限包括：未明确讨论该方法在极大规模网络或极端低比特（如二值化）下的表现，以及TSC和BIC两模块联合训练时可能引入的超参数敏感性。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) encode information through binary spikes and compute in an event-driven manner, offering an energy-efficient paradigm for machine intelligence. However, high-performance SNNs incur substantial memory and timestep-wise computation costs that hinder deployment on resource-constrained devices. Quantization and pruning provide complementary routes to reducing these costs, yet both make their decisions with local criteria that overlook temporal task feedback in quantization and inter-channel dependencies in pruning. Consequently, optimizing either criterion can still yield suboptimal compression performance. We refer to this discrepancy as criterion-behavior mismatch and propose Behavior-Aligned SNN Compression (BASC), a unified framework with two lightweight modules. For quantization, the scale is applied to synaptic current at every timestep and therefore shifts spike timing. Temporal-Behavior Scale Correction (TSC) makes the scale learnable under a temporal loss, allowing firing behavior to inform scale optimization. For pruning, channel importance depends on how channels jointly drive the membrane potential across the firing threshold. Boundary-Level Inter-Channel Correction (BIC) uses channelwise importance scores for initial selection and inter-channel information to re-evaluate only channels near the pruning threshold. Extensive experiments on static and neuromorphic benchmarks show that lower-bit BASC models match or outperform higher-bit baselines and retain this accuracy advantage after structured pruning, while further reducing model storage and synaptic operations.

</details>


### [70] [The Forward-Backward Disconnect: State Dynamics, Credit Assignment, and Biological Grounding in Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19995)
*Hadi Al Mubasher,Mariette Awad*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文揭示并形式化了一个神经计算中的关键不对称现象——"前向-反向断连"（forward-backward disconnect）：前向计算在结构上已极大多样化（涵盖递归、注意力、状态空间、连续时间、隐式均衡、脉冲等模型族），而可扩展的训练机制仍高度集中于全局反向传播及其衍生方法（BPTT、伴随方法、隐式微分、代理梯度等）。论文提出一个跨越三个耦合轴（状态动力学结构、信用分配机制、生物基础）的分类框架，以"架构-学习配置"而非架构名称作为分析单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对的核心问题在于：尽管前向计算（forward computation）已从早期前馈网络的简化抽象演化为多种具有动态和生物结构的模型族，但可扩展的训练（training）却始终局限于反向传播类机制。这种前向与反向发展的不对称性限制了模型的表达力与训练效率的协同进化。厘清这一"前向-反向断连"极具重要性，因为它关乎深度学习理论、生物可塑性学习（如SNN训练）以及高效AI系统设计等多个领域的发展方向——若信用分配机制无法与前向动力学结构对齐，则难以充分挖掘新架构的潜力。

Method: 论文的核心方法论贡献在于提出一个系统化的分类框架（taxonomy），沿三条相互耦合的轴对神经模型族进行分类：1）状态动力学结构（state-dynamics structure）：涵盖静态、递归、注意力机制、状态空间、连续时间、隐式均衡以及脉冲等前向计算形式；2）信用分配机制（credit-assignment mechanism）：包括反向传播、时间反向传播（BPTT）、伴随方法、隐式微分和代理梯度（surrogate-gradient）等训练方式；3）生物基础（biological grounding）：将前向计算基础与学习的基础分开独立考察。关键创新在于"以架构-学习配置（architecture-learning configuration）为分析单元"——即强调应同时考察模型结构与训练机制的组合，而非孤立地按架构名称划分。

Result: 论文通过对静态网络、递归网络、基于注意力的模型、状态空间模型、连续时间模型、隐式模型、脉冲神经网络、生物可塑性模型及神经形态（neuromorphic）模型族进行系统分析后发现：前向动力学在上述各模型族中已实现显著的多样化，但"最高演示规模"（highest demonstrated scales）仍集中于全局或强梯度衍生的误差传播机制。这一实证观察支持论文的核心论断，即存在前向与反向发展的结构性不对称。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是：1）提出并明确定义"前向-反向断连"这一概念，指出前向计算多样化与训练机制单一化之间的不对称；2）构建了一个连接架构类型、学习算法与生物基础三者关系的分类学框架；3）强调以"架构-学习配置"而非传统模型类别作为分析单元，为未来研究提供了更精确的对比基准。其核心启示是：要弥合这一断连，需要实现状态动力学、信用分配机制与计算基质（如神经形态硬件）之间更好的对齐。论文属视角性/综述性研究，局限性在于其分析主要基于已有文献的规模证据，未提出具体的新学习算法；同时"生物基础"轴的定义可能与具体神经科学诠释存在歧义。

Abstract: A recurring pattern in neural computation is the reintroduction of dynamical and biological structure into models originally simplified for scalable optimization. Early feedforward networks reduced biological neurons to threshold or rate-like summation units, an abstraction compatible with global-gradient training at scale. Since then, forward computation has diversified: modern architectures carry recurrent state, retrieve from long contexts through attention and associative memory, compress histories through structured state-space dynamics, evolve in continuous time, settle to implicit equilibria, and communicate through sparse spikes.
  Training has diversified less. Scalable learning remains concentrated around backpropagation, backpropagation through time, adjoint methods, implicit differentiation, and surrogate-gradient variants. We call this asymmetry the forward-backward disconnect and develop a taxonomy spanning neural model families along three coupled axes: state-dynamics structure, credit-assignment mechanism, and biological grounding. Forward and learning grounding are treated separately, and the unit of analysis is the architecture-learning configuration rather than the architecture name alone.
  Across static, recurrent, attention-based, state-space, continuous-time, implicit, spiking, biologically plausible, and neuromorphic families, forward dynamics have diversified while the highest demonstrated scales remain concentrated in global or closely gradient-derived error-propagation mechanisms. Closing this disconnect requires better alignment among state dynamics, credit assignment, and computational substrate.

</details>


### [71] [Petri Net Description of Biological Neural Circuits for Fast Hardware Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20147)
*Carlo daCunha,Rodrigo Pena,Marcos Turqueti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出用 T-timed 彼得网（Petri net）对生物神经回路进行建模，将神经元、突触和脉冲事件表示为具有形式化可验证时间语义的定时网络，从而克服传统定点步长数值积分和硬件精度限制的不足，实现具有截止时间（deadline）保证的实时脉冲动力学执行，并保持与连续时间 LIF（leaky integrate-and-fire）动力学的解析对应关系。通过反馈抑制、侧向抑制和分层特征检测三个微回路的仿真验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物神经回路仿真方法（包括通用硬件和专用神经形态平台）存在三大固有局限：①采用固定时间步长的数值积分，无法精确捕捉事件驱动的脉冲时序；②受限于硬件精度（如定点/浮点位数），影响数值准确性；③无法在实时约束下为事件驱动脉冲动力学提供时序正确性的形式化保证。这在需要硬实时保障的神经形态计算、机器人感知和实时事件驱动系统中尤为关键，因此需要一种能够提供定时正确性证明、同时保持与连续时间神经动力学解析对应关系的建模框架。

Method: 核心创新在于将生物神经回路抽象为 T-timed 彼得网（T-timed Petri Net）：将神经元、突触和脉冲事件分别映射为网络的库所（place）与变迁（transition），通过变迁的定时延迟（timing delay）对突触传播和脉冲发放过程显式建模。该方法具备两个关键特性：(1) 形式化可验证的时间语义——可实现 deadline-guaranteed 的实时执行，即每个脉冲事件的响应时间都有形式化上界；(2) 解析可处理的对应关系——该定时网模型与连续时间 leak-integrate-and-fire（LIF）动力学之间具有解析可推导的对应，且该对应独立于底层积分时间步长。这为事件驱动脉冲活动的实时执行提供了数学上可证明的时序保证，弥补了传统数值积分方法无法给出形式化实时权衡的缺陷。

Result: 作者在三种代表性微回路上对模型进行了验证：(1) 反馈抑制（feedback inhibition）、(2) 侧向抑制（lateral inhibition）、(3) 分层特征检测器（hierarchical feature detector）。实验结果（基于仿真）表明：Petri 神经元在三种回路中均能复现各回路预期的动力学特征/签名（dynamical signatures），同时整个过程提供形式化的有界时序保证；三种情形下最坏情况响应时间（worst-case response time）均与分析预测一致。这证明模型既能保持生物回路的动力学行为，又能提供可解析验证的实时性能上限。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是为实时神经回路仿真提出了一种新颖的、具备形式化可验证时间语义的 T-timed 彼得网建模框架，解决了传统定点步长积分在实时保证和时序正确性方面的根本缺陷，同时通过解析对应关系与连续 LIF 动力学相连，实现了"动力学保真+实时可证明"的统一。其意义在于为神经形态计算、事件驱动感知和硬实时机器人系统提供了一种可验证的建模与执行范式。局限在于：目前仅在三个相对简单的微回路仿真中验证，尚未在复杂大规模网络、真实神经形态硬件或实际事件相机/机器人感知任务中测试；其与 LIF 的解析对应在更复杂神经动态（如适应、突触可塑性）下的扩展性也有待探索。

Abstract: Current approaches to simulating biological neural circuits, whether on general-purpose hardware or dedicated neuromorphic platforms, remain constrained by fixed-timestep numerical integration, hardware-imposed precision limits, and an inability to guarantee timing correctness for event-driven spiking dynamics under real-time constraints. Here, we propose a Petri net description of biological neural circuits that overcomes these limitations by modeling neurons, synapses, and spike events as a T-timed Petri net with formally verifiable timing semantics, enabling deadline-guaranteed real-time execution and analytically tractable correspondence to continuous-time leak-integrate-and-fire dynamics, independent of the underlying integration timestep. To test the model, we present the results of three simulated microcircuits: feedback inhibition, lateral inhibition, and hierarchical feature detector. The Petri neuron reproduces the expected dynamical signatures of each circuit while providing formally bounded timing guarantees throughout, with worst-case response times matching analytical predictions across all three cases.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [APPROVE: Visual End-User-in-the-Loop Robot Programming with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19281)
*Bijan Kavousian,Miray Özakkas,Josefine Monnet,Oliver Petrovic,Christian Brecher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 APPROVE——一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的多模态终端用户机器人编程框架，将自然语言输入与 Blockly 块状编程界面及显式用户确认步骤相结合。生成的程序以块状形式可视化，用户可在执行前确认、修改或拒绝；确认的函数被存入可复用组件库，逐步积累可靠的程序模块。核心贡献是强调用户信任、意图对齐和可复用性的人本化 LLM 机器人编程设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人编程对非专家用户仍具挑战性，传统方法需要专业知识，而主流模块化（block-based）界面虽然降低门槛但缺乏灵活性。近年来基于 LLM 自动从自然语言生成机器人程序的方法存在三大局限：缺乏可解释性/透明度（用户难以理解生成逻辑）、缺少确保程序与用户意图对齐的机制（可能生成与意图不符的程序）、以及对已生成程序的复用支持不足（每次需从头生成）。这些问题直接关系到用户信任度与系统的实际可用性。

Method: APPROVE 采用人机协作的闭环框架，技术要点包括：(1) 多模态输入融合——将自然语言指令与块状编程界面相结合作为输入；(2) LLM 程序生成——由 LLM 从多模态输入中自动生成机器人程序；(3) Blockly 可视化呈现——生成的程序被转换为块状可视化形式，提供明确的语义层级结构，使非专家用户能够理解程序逻辑；(4) 显式用户确认环节——用户可在执行前确认、修改或拒绝程序，构建意图对齐机制；(5) 复用组件库——被确认的可靠程序片段存入函数库，支持逐步积累和复用，减少重复生成并提升可靠性与一致性。

Result: 论文主要从系统设计与人机交互角度展开，阐述了 APPROVE 的框架架构和工作流程。强调该设计通过可视化块状程序（而非黑盒代码）赋予用户审查与干预能力，以可验证的方式增强了用户信任；通过显式确认环节保障程序和用户意图的对齐；通过组件库实现跨任务的程序复用。研究作为设计论证（design argument）性质的工作，重点呈现了系统结构与交互范式，而非大规模定量实验结果。

Conclusion: APPROVE 的主要贡献是为 LLM 驱动的机器人编程提出了一种人本化设计范式，通过自然语言+块状界面+确认+复用库的组合，针对性地解决了现有 LLM 编程系统透明度不足、意图对齐缺失和复用支持薄弱三大问题。其意义在于将"可审查、可干预、可积累"的人机协作原则显式纳入 LLM 程序生成流程，提升非专家用户的信任度与可控性。限制在于：论文缺乏系统的用户研究和定量评估数据（如可用性、成功率与用户满意度），组件库的积累机制、LLM 生成错误的处理策略以及框架在真实机器人平台上的端到端验证仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Programming robots remains challenging for non-experts, as traditional methods require expert knowledge and even block-based interfaces often lack flexibility. Recent work has explored Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate robot programs from natural language, but these systems remain limited by a lack of transparency, missing mechanisms to ensure alignment with user intent, and little support for reuse. We present APPROVE (AI-Powered Programming for Robots with Visual End-User Feedback), an LLM-based multi-modal end-user programming framework that integrates natural language input with a block-based interface and an explicit user confirmation step. Generated programs are visualized using a block-based interface in Blockly, allowing users to confirm, modify, or reject them before execution. Confirmed functions are stored in a library for reuse, gradually building a set of reliable program components. Our approach contributes a human-centered design for LLM-based robot programming that emphasizes user trust, intent alignment, and reusability.

</details>


### [73] [The Missing Touch: Spatially Distributed Tactile Feedback Brings Teleoperation Closer to Human Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19372)
*Rohan Kota,Gregory Reardon,J. Edward Colgate*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人遥操作中的人机灵巧度差距问题，通过配备32自由度假指尖显示器的双自由度双边力反馈遥操作系统，证明空间分布式触觉反馈是缩小这一差距的关键因素。实验表明，复现局部接触形变可将遥操作与自然人运动轨迹的偏差降低29%-79%，并压缩遥操作运动的状态空间分布，有利于提升自主机器人策略的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作是收集机器人自主策略训练数据的重要途径，但当前遥操作系统的操作性能远低于人类手部的灵巧度，即使对基本任务也是如此。作者提出一个关键假设：这种性能差距的根源在于缺乏空间分布式的触觉反馈——传统遥操作系统通常只提供力反馈（如单点阻力），无法传递接触在指尖上的空间局部分布信息。这一问题对灵巧操作至关重要，因为人类在执行精细操作时高度依赖皮肤上的分布式接触感。理解并弥补这一感知缺失将直接关系到位姿去程遥操作数据质量和自主机器人策略训练效果。

Method: 系统由两部分构成：(1) 一个2自由度双边力反馈遥操作机械臂，用于远程操作；(2) 一个32自由度假指尖触觉显示设备，用于将远端机械臂上的局部形变以高分辨率方式复现在操作者指尖。核心技术创新在于对测量位移进行细粒度量化（量化分辨率可调的精细程度）来再现分布式接触信息。通过调整触觉反馈分辨率，作者能控制遥操作运动的状态空间压缩程度——更高的触觉分辨率使遥操作运动更加集中和确定性，这与改善自主策略训练效果相关联。该方法实现了从"单点力反馈"到"空间分布式触觉再现"的感知范式转变。

Result: 在一系列遥操作任务中验证了效果：(1) 复现分布式接触信息显著加速任务完成速度；(2) 减少了修正动作和任务完成步骤数，使操作行为更接近自然人类行为；(3) 遥操作轨迹与自然人类轨迹的偏差降低29%-79%；(4) 提高触觉反馈分辨率（即更精细地量化位移再现）可压缩遥操作运动的状态空间分布，该压缩已被研究证明有利于自主机器人策略的训练收敛和质量。(5) 遥操作运动的运动学特征（修正动作、步骤数、轨迹偏差）更接近自然人类操作的各项指示性指标。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于通过严格的系统对比实验，证实了空间分布式触觉反馈是弥合人类灵巧操作与遥操作性能之间差距的核心因素，而非传统观点中的力反馈精度或延迟等次要因素。这一发现对遥操作系统设计和遥控数据驱动机器人学习（如模仿学习、示教学习）具有重要意义：高分辨率分布式触觉反馈不仅提升操作者体验和效率，还通过压缩遥操作运动状态空间来改善下游自主策略的训练。尽管研究中通过量化分辨率变化来推演训练收益，但并未直接在下游策略训练上进行全流程验证，局限于遥操作运动学和任务执行层面的证据，这是可能的局限。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in robotic teleoperation is enabling an operator to control a remote robot as effortlessly and intuitively as their own hands. Despite the growing use of teleoperation to collect demonstration data for training autonomous robot policies, teleoperated robot performance still falls significantly short of human dexterity, even for basic tasks. Here, we present evidence that a key factor contributing to this performance gap is the absence of spatially distributed tactile feedback. Using a two-degree-of-freedom (DoF) bilateral force-feedback telemanipulator paired with a 32-DoF tactile fingertip display, we show that operator performance improves significantly when localized deformations on the remote manipulator are faithfully reproduced on the operator's fingertip. In a series of teleoperation tasks, reproducing distributed contact information not only accelerated task performance but also brought teleoperated movements closer to natural human behavior by minimizing corrective actions and task completion steps, thereby reducing the deviation between teleoperated and natural trajectories by 29$\unicode{x2013}$79%. Furthermore, we found that increasing the resolution of the tactile feedback$\unicode{x2014}$by refining how finely the measured displacements were quantized for reproduction$\unicode{x2014}$compressed the state-space distribution of teleoperated motions, which has been associated with improved training outcomes for autonomous robot policies. Together, these results suggest that spatially distributed tactile feedback is essential for closing the gap between human and teleoperated dexterity and training the next generation of autonomous robots.

</details>


### [74] [Learning the Right Abstraction: Neural Reduced Dynamics for Complex Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19375)
*Harry Zhang,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出神经约化动力学（Neural Reduced Dynamics, NRD）框架，主张复杂机器人控制中神经动力学模型的价值在于学习"正确的抽象"（right abstraction）——即保留控制相关物理特性的约化状态。NRD将模型传播的状态与可供给输入或解析恢复的量分离，在冻结的学习模型内部训练策略，再回到高保真模拟器中验证。在履带车辆轨迹跟踪和机械臂目标到达等任务中，所有策略均可迁移回高保真模拟器，且模拟速度比高保真场景快约4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真具身AI模拟器能为复杂机器人系统提供真实评估，但其计算成本极大，限制了其直接用于大规模强化学习（RL）训练。这一问题至关重要，因为现实机器人学习中策略需要在大量交互样本中迭代，而高保真物理仿真无法提供所需的吞吐量。作者因此主张改用精度较低但速度更快的仿真——例如数据驱动的神经动力学模型。其核心动机是：神经动力学模型对复杂机器人控制的实际价值不在于精确复现全部物理过程，而在于学习正确的状态抽象，即一个约化状态，既保留高保真系统中与控制相关的物理特性，又能支撑高吞吐量的策略学习。

Method: NRD框架包含三项核心设计：(1) 状态分离——明确区分模型实际传播的状态与可外部供给的输入（如地形信息）或可解析恢复的量（如运动学量），从而降低模型学习负担并保持物理一致性；(2) 冻结训练——策略完全在冻结（frozen）的学习得到的约化动力学模型内部进行训练，无需接触昂贵的高保真模拟器；(3) 迁移验证——训练后的策略回到高保真模拟器中进行验证，确保物理真实性。框架通过三个控制任务实例化：地形感知的HMMWV（高机动多用途轮式车）在刚性、颠簸及可变形连续介质地形（CRM）上的轨迹跟踪；以及履带式车辆及其前装关节臂的目标到达任务。关键创新在于地形条件化的动力学模型，其策略本身不接收地形输入，却能在所有三种地形上取得优于单一地形专家模型的性能。

Result: 实验涵盖三类控制任务：地形感知的HMMWV轨迹跟踪（刚性、颠簸、可变形CRM地形）、履带式车辆目标到达、以及前装关节臂目标到达。主要结果：(1) 所有策略均成功从冻结的NRD模型迁移回高保真模拟器；(2) 在地形条件化动力学模型内部训练、自身不接收地形输入的策略，在全部三种地形上均获得比单一地形专家更低的跟踪误差中位数和均值，包括在颠簸地形上的零样本泛化；(3) 履带式车辆实现100/100目标到达，关节臂实现97/100目标到达，且无任何接触或关节限位违规；(4) NRD模型相比其替代的高保真模拟场景，模拟时间推进速度约为4个数量级（约10^4倍），使迭代式在线策略学习变得可行。

Conclusion: 本论文的主要贡献是提出并实证了"正确抽象"原则：神经约化动力学模型对复杂机器人控制的价值在于学习约化状态，而非精确复制高保真仿真。NRD框架通过状态分离、冻结策略训练和迁移验证三层设计，成功将离线学习的低成本动力学模型与高保真物理验证结合，实现了约4个数量级的模拟加速，为大规模机器人策略学习与精确物理仿真之间架起了一座实用桥梁。其局限性在于：论文本身是观点论证型（advocacy）贡献，案例覆盖的机器人和任务类型有限，且未深入讨论NRD模型在极端未知地形或高动态场景下的泛化边界以及学习NRD模型所需的训练数据获取成本；对地形条件化模型的零样本泛化机制也未给出理论性解释。

Abstract: High-fidelity embodied AI simulators provide realistic evaluation of complex robotic systems, but their computational cost limits their direct use for large-scale reinforcement learning campaigns. We advocate the use of less accurate but more expeditious simulations, which might draw on data-driven, e.g., neural dynamics, models. This contribution argues that the practical value of a neural dynamics model for complex robot control lies in learning the \emph{right abstraction}: a reduced state that preserves the control-relevant physics of the high-fidelity system while enabling high-throughput policy learning. We develop a neural reduced dynamics (NRD) framework that separates the state the model propagates from what can be supplied as an input or recovered analytically, trains policies entirely inside the frozen learned model, and validates them back in the high-fidelity simulator. Two case studies instantiate it across three control tasks: terrain-aware HMMWV trajectory tracking on rigid, bumpy and deformable Continuum Representation Model (CRM) terrain; and goal reaching for a stock tracked vehicle and its front-mounted articulated arm. Every policy transfers back to the high-fidelity simulator. A single policy trained inside the terrain-conditioned dynamics model, and given no terrain input of its own, attains lower median and mean tracking error than both single-terrain specialists on all three terrains, including zero-shot bumpy terrain. Quantitatively, the tracked vehicle reaches 100 of 100 goals and the arm 97 of 100, with zero contacts or joint-limit violations. The NRD models advance roughly four orders of magnitude faster in simulated time than the high-fidelity simulator scenes they replace, making iterative on-policy learning practical and supporting neural reduced dynamics as a bridge between accurate but expensive physics simulation and scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [75] [SCAPE: Scenario-Conditioned Simulation-Augmented Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19425)
*Dijie Zhu,Seunghun Oh,Ruopeng Huang,Zhiyu Huang,Jiaqi Ma,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAPE是一种场景条件化的仿真增强策略评估框架（Scenario-Conditioned Simulation-Augmented Policy Evaluation），用于在真实世界部署前预测机器人学习策略的场景条件化性能。它利用有限的配对仿真-真实样本和大规模仿真rollout来校正模拟到真实的偏差，并通过保形预测校准预测不确定性。在自动驾驶和Unitree Go2四足机器人速度跟踪任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠评估是机器人学习策略部署的核心瓶颈。真实世界测试忠实但成本高昂且难以规模化；仿真测试易扩展但受sim-to-real差距偏差影响。现有仿真增强方法仅在初始条件和部署设置的总体平均性能上进行评估，这种总体平均掩盖了场景特异性的性能变化，无法指导策略在何时何地可安全部署。因此亟需一种能提供场景条件化性能预测、且能处理sim-to-real偏差和不确定性校准的评估框架。

Method: SCAPE框架的核心技术流程包括三部分：首先利用有限的配对sim-and-real样本，对大规模仿真rollout中的仿真标签进行sim-to-real偏差校正，修正仿真数据的系统性偏差；然后在校正后的仿真数据上训练场景条件化的性能预测模型，实现对特定场景真实性能的预测；最后通过保形预测（conformal prediction）对预测结果进行不确定性校准，生成校准后的预测区间。该方法将场景级信息纳入评估，而非传统的总体平均，可支持细粒度的部署策略。

Result: 在自动驾驶和四足速度跟踪两项任务上验证。Sim-to-sim研究中，SCAPE在驾驶任务上相对于场景条件神经基线和聚合统计基线分别将场景级预测误差降低4.9%/34.7%，在四足任务上降低14.5%/27.7%。此外，在物理Unitree Go2机器人上部署了一个速度跟踪策略进行真实评估。SCAPE还提升了测试样本效率，产生更窄的校准预测区间，对分布外场景具有更好的泛化能力，并支持细粒度部署策略。

Conclusion: SCAPE的主要贡献在于将策略评估从总体平均转向场景条件化预测，通过sim-to-real标签校正和保形不确定性校准，实现了可靠且样本高效的策略评估。其意义在于为真实环境中的安全部署提供了可量化的场景级保障。可能的局限包括：需要有限的配对sim-and-real样本、对预测模型的依赖、以及在不同任务和更高维决策空间的扩展性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Reliable performance evaluation is a central bottleneck for deploying robot-learning policies in real-world conditions. Real-world testing is faithful but costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation-based testing scales easily but is inevitably biased by the sim-to-real gap. Existing simulation-augmented methods combine limited real-world rollouts with abundant simulation proxies, but focus on performance averaged over initial conditions and deployment settings. Such population-level averages obscure scenario-specific variation and provide limited guidance about when and where a policy can be safely deployed. We propose SCAPE, a scenario-conditioned simulation-augmented policy evaluation framework that predicts scenario-conditioned real-world policy performance using limited paired sim-and-real samples and large-scale simulation rollouts. SCAPE corrects sim-to-real bias in simulation labels before training the prediction model and calibrates prediction uncertainty through conformal prediction. We validate SCAPE on autonomous driving and quadruped velocity tracking. In sim-to-sim studies, SCAPE reduces scenario-level prediction error by 4.9%/34.7% (driving) and 14.5%/27.7% (quadruped) relative to scene-conditioned neural and aggregate statistical baselines on average. We further evaluate a velocity-tracking policy deployed on a physical Unitree Go2. SCAPE also improves testing sample efficiency, produces narrower calibrated prediction intervals, generalizes better to out-of-distribution scenarios, and enables fine-grained deployment strategies.

</details>


### [76] [Hybrid Feedback Sampling for Sample-Efficient Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19443)
*Chaoyi Pan,Zeji Yi,John Zhang,Zachary Manchester,Guannan Qu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈采样模型预测控制（FS-MPC），通过将最优采样提议分布建模为带优化的反馈策略，并采用混合采样设计方案（根据系统稳定性和计算预算在局部与全局搜索间平衡），解决了采样型MPC在面向高维、开环不稳定系统时样本效率低和数值不稳定（shooting方法不稳定）的问题。理论分析和接触丰富任务实验显示FS-MPC比标准MPPI收敛更快、比标准反馈采样最优性更好，并在真实人形机器人上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型MPC（如MPPI类方法）因并行化和灵活性强而广泛应用于真实机器人控制，但其在面向高维、开环不稳定动态系统时存在根本缺陷：由于shooting方法的数值不稳定，为在时间视界上改善控制序列所需的采样数会随视界长度呈指数增长，导致样本效率低下和数值不稳定。本文旨在从理论上阐明shooting方法不稳定性的成因，并提出一种兼顾样本效率和最优性的采样策略，使采样型MPC能够应对动态不稳定且接触丰富的复杂任务（如人形机器人操作）。

Method: FS-MPC的核心创新包括：（1）理论分析表明，最优采样提议分布可以通过以优化后的反馈策略进行采样来实现；（2）提出混合采样设计，在局部搜索（围绕当前反馈策略的紧密采样）与全局搜索（扩大探索范围）之间平衡，这一权衡依据系统稳定的程度和可用计算预算动态调整；（3）从理论上证明该混合采样方法比标准MPPI收敛更快，比标准反馈采样方法具有更好的最优性。该方法本质上是将反馈控制策略嵌入采样过程，从根本上缓解了shooting方法在长视界下的指数样本需求。

Result: 实验覆盖多样化的接触丰富控制任务：（1）理论验证了混合采样比标准MPPI收敛更快、比标准反馈采样最优性更优；（2）在人形机器人locomotion-manipulation（行走进操作）和灵巧操作（dexterous manipulation）等动态不稳定任务中，FS-MPC成功处理了标准样本方法难以应对的场景，并严格优于单独使用反馈策略的方法；（3）在真实世界的真人形机器人行走与操作任务上进行了物理验证。论文以收敛速度、最优性指标以及成功处理不稳定任务的能力作为主要评估标准。

Conclusion: FS-MPC的主要贡献在于：从理论上揭示了采样型MPC中shooting方法不稳定的机理，将反馈策略与采样提议分布相结合构建了混合采样框架，在收敛速度与最优性之间取得平衡，并首次在真实人形机器人上验证了该方法对动态不稳定接触丰富任务的有效性。其意义在于为采样型MPC的样本效率问题提供了新的理论理解和实用解决方案，使采样优化能够扩展到高维、开环不稳定的系统。可能的局限包括：方法的性能依赖对系统稳定性程度的评估与计算预算的设定，混合权衡的超参数调参较复杂，以及理论分析主要针对特定类别的动态系统，推广到更广泛系统的泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Thanks to its parallelizability and flexibility, sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) has become widely popular for controlling real-world robotic systems. However, for high-dimensional and open-loop unstable dynamical systems, the required number of samples to improve the control sequence will grow exponentially with the horizon, leading to poor sample efficiency and numerical instability. This paper investigates the instability of shooting methods in sampling-based MPC and shows that the optimal sampling proposal distribution can be realized by sampling with an optimized feedback policy. We refer to this algorithm as Feedback Sampling MPC (FS-MPC). FS-MPC involves a hybrid sampling design which balances local and global search based on the system stability and the available computation budget. Our theoretical analysis shows that our hybrid sampling approach achieves faster convergence than standard MPPI and better optimality than standard feedback sampling. Empirically, in diverse contact-rich control tasks like humanoid loco-manipulation and dexterous manipulation, we show that FS-MPC successfully tackles dynamically unstable tasks where standard sample-based approaches struggle, and strictly outperforms feedback policies alone. Finally, we validate our method on humanoid robot locomotion and manipulation tasks in the real world.

</details>


### [77] [When Automata Meet Streams: Temporal Logic Compilation for Stream-Based Robotics Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19453)
*Sayem Nazmuz Zaman,Cyrus Neary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAM-TD 是一种编译方法，用于在基于流（stream-based）的任务与运动规划（TAMP）中强制执行任意的有限迹线性时序逻辑（LTL_f）约束。它将 LTL_f 约束编译为自动机，并将回归的自动机守卫嵌入到动作模式中，通过同步更新自动机状态和共享的“有效性令牌”（validity token）实现约束违规分支的剪枝，从而支持规划过程中由流动态生成的对象。实验首次在三个 PDDLStream 机器人环境中展示了流式 TAMP 在 LTL_f 约束下的可行性，并在标准离散 PDDL 基准上与最先进的时序约束编译方法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 TAMP 将离散符号规划与动态生成的连续几何参数（位姿、抓取、轨迹）相结合。然而，现有流式规划器通常只推理目标可达性，而长期任务还需要满足时间规范，如安全性排序、不变性和活性约束。由于流在规划期间通过迭代精化循环生成不断扩展的几何对象集，现有的时序逻辑编译技术（需要枚举固定对象集）无法直接用于流式求解器。此前没有任何方法能在流式求解器中强制此类时序约束，这是本工作的核心动机。

Method: SAM-TD 将任意的 LTL_f 约束转换为自动机，并将回归的自动机守卫嵌入到预先指定（planning前已知）的动作模式（action schema）中，从而避免在规划开始前枚举固定对象集。在搜索过程中，SAM-TD 同步更新自动机状态，并使用跨所有自动机共享的有效性令牌（validity token）来剪除违反约束的分支。这种设计使得 SAM-TD 能够处理由流在迭代精化过程中动态生成的对象，无需修改底层规划器。

Result: 实验首次在三个 PDDLStream 机器人环境中展示了流式 TAMP 在 LTL_f 约束下的运行。此外，在标准离散 PDDL 基准上，SAM-TD 与最先进的时序约束编译方法（state-of-the-art temporal-constraint compilation methods）性能相当。

Conclusion: SAM-TD 的主要贡献在于首次实现了流式 TAMP 框架下的 LTL_f 时序约束满足，通过自动机守卫嵌入、同步状态更新和共享有效性令牌剪枝三大机制，克服了流生成动态对象带来的编译难题，且不依赖修改底层规划器。其意义在于将时序规范推理引入流式 TAMP，扩展了其适用范围。可能的局限性包括对复杂 LTL_f 约束的可扩展性、自动机状态爆炸问题，以及实验覆盖的机器人场景相对有限（三个 PDDLStream 环境）。

Abstract: Stream-based robotics Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) integrates discrete symbolic planning with dynamically generated continuous geometric parameters, such as poses, grasps, and trajectories. However, stream-based planners typically reason only about goal reachability, whereas long-horizon tasks also demand adherence to temporal specifications, such as safety-critical ordering, invariance, and liveness constraints. No methods currently exist to enforce such temporal constraints for stream-based solvers because streams generate an expanding geometric object set via iterative stream refinement loops during planning, rendering existing temporal-logic compilation techniques incompatible. We therefore present Synchronous Action Monitoring with Token Destruction (SAM-TD), a compilation method that enforces arbitrary Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\textrm{LTL}_f$) specifications in stream-based TAMP. SAM-TD translates arbitrary $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints into automata and embeds regressed automaton guards into action schemas, which are pre-specified before planning begins. By doing so, SAM-TD can handle objects generated by streams during planning, thus circumventing the need to enumerate a fixed object set or modify the underlying planner. During search, SAM-TD synchronously updates automaton states and uses a validity token shared across all automata to prune constraint-violating branches. We show that SAM-TD supports dynamically generated stream objects from iterative stream refinements during plan search. Experimental results provide the first ever demonstration of stream-based TAMP under $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints in three robotics PDDLStream environments. Furthermore, on standard discrete PDDL benchmarks, SAM-TD is competitive with state-of-the-art temporal-constraint compilation methods.

</details>


### [78] [Fine-Tuning VLAs with Self-Demonstrated Generative Control for Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19490)
*Prachi Garg,Steve Xing,Prahit Yaugand,Saurabh Gupta,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督微调方法用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型到新机器人本体的迁移。该方法利用零样本VLA模型生成在线交互rollout作为额外训练数据，与领域内专家数据联合微调，使目标机器人上的策略既能继承预训练先验任务、保持通用指令跟随能力，又能以更高样本效率学习新技能。在真实ALOHA机器人和RoboTwin新仿真基准上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型（如π₀.₅）在语义理解、指令跟随和任务行为上表现优异，但在部署到新机器人时，即使硬件配置与预训练设置存在微小差异，也会导致严重性能下降。常见的解决方法是在新本体的领域内专家数据上微调VLA，但这种方式在提升专家任务性能的同时会破坏模型原有的指令跟随能力和行为先验，造成灾难性遗忘。因此，需要一种既能学习新技能又不丢失原有能力的高效微调方案，这对于VLA在多样化机器人平台上的实际部署具有重要意义。

Method: 核心方案是一种自监督微调框架。具体而言：（1）利用零样本（zero-shot）VLA模型在目标机器人上生成在线交互rollout数据，而非依赖昂贵的专家数据采集；（2）将这些自生成的rollout与领域内专家数据联合用于VLA微调，构建多任务训练集；（3）该方案旨在实现三个目标：从零样本模型蒸馏继承原始任务能力、保持通用指令跟随能力、同时从专家数据中高效学习新技能。该方法无需额外标注，属于自监督数据生成+联合微调的范式，通过自生成数据作为先验知识的载体来缓解微调中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 实验在两条验证路径上进行：（1）真实ALOHA机器人上的测试集，用于探针测试泛化能力；（2）RoboTwin中构建的新仿真基准。实验结果显示该方法相比单纯在专家数据上微调，能同时满足三个目标：在目标机器人上继承零样本模型蒸馏的先前任务、保持通用指令跟随能力、并以更高的样本效率从专家数据中学习新技能。多任务策略在两种平台上均表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于提出了一种无需额外标注的自监督微调策略，通过将零样本VLA的在线交互数据作为训练信号，有效解决了VLA迁移到新机器人时的能力遗忘问题，实现了先验保留与新技能学习的双重目标。同时证明了其在真实机器人和仿真环境中的泛化有效性。局限在于采样效率、自生成数据的质量影响以及rollout策略的稳定性仍需进一步验证，且实验主要集中在ALOHA这一具体本体上，对更多样化机器人平台的泛化性有待探讨。

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_{0.5}$ exhibit strong semantic understanding, instruction following and task behavior. However, when deployed on new robots, even minor mismatches in hardware configuration relative to pretraining can cause severe performance drops. Finetuning the VLA on in-domain expert data from the new embodiment improves performance on the expert task but leads to a loss in its original instruction following and behavioral priors. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised method that generates online interaction rollouts from the zero-shot VLA as additional training data for finetuning. Our experiments show this finetuning scheme yields strong multi-task policies that, on the target robot, (1) inherit prior tasks distilled from the zero-shot model, (2) enable generalist instruction following, while (3) learning new skills from expert data with improved sample efficiency. We demonstrate the success of our approach across test sets probing generalization on a real ALOHA robot and a new simulation benchmark in RoboTwin. Video results are available at https://self-supervised-control.pages.dev/

</details>


### [79] [LF-GICP: Parameter-Free Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR Odometry via a Voxel-Normal Localizability Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19522)
*Eunsoo Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数无关（parameter-free）的激光雷达里程计退化处理方案 LF-GICP。作者发现体素化 GICP 中高斯-牛顿 Hessian 会掩盖平移方向的退化（因协方差正则化使平移块人为良态），因此提出无正则化的体素法线局域化场（voxel-normal localizability field）及其两种统计量：归一化分数 f₀（检测方向各向异性）和逐体素绝对质量 λ₀（区分信息缺失的隧道与信息稀释的开阔场景），并通过时间中值门控结合两者触发 Fisher 信息对应加权。该方法只需在两条短序列上用固定规则校准一次后冻结，即在 KITTI、GEODE 隧道、MulRan、HeLiPR 等多个数据集与四种传感器类型上达到最优或领先精度，并实证证明笔直均匀隧道沿其轴向对激光雷达配准本质不可观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光雷达扫描配准/里程计在几何退化环境中（如隧道、走廊）会沿不可观轴产生无界漂移。现有退化处理方法需要针对场景进行参数调整，缺乏通用性和适应性。本文旨在解决两个关键问题：(1) 揭示体素化 GICP 中 GN Hessian 因协方差正则化而无法反映平移退化这一机理缺陷；(2) 提出无需人工调参、可在多类型传感器与场景间泛化的退化感知方案，这对于自动驾驶、机器人导航等真实应用至关重要。

Method: 核心技术创新包括：(1) 机理分析：证明在体素化 GICP 中 GN Hessian 的平移块因协方差正则化被人为保持良态，从而掩盖了平移方向的退化；(2) 提出无正则化的体素法线局域化场（voxel-normal localizability field）作为替代表示；(3) 构造两个统计量：归一化分数 f₀（检测方向各向异性/退化轴向）和绝对逐体素质量 λ₀（区分信息缺失的隧道与信息稀释而密集的开阔场景）；(4) 时间中值门控（temporal-median gate）组合 f₀ 与 λ₀，触发基于 Fisher 信息的对应加权策略来抑制退化方向的影响；(5) 整个流程仅用固定规则在两条短序列上校准一次后即冻结，无需按场景调参，可跨四种传感器类型泛化。

Result: 在统一评估协议下对重新运行的基线（re-run baselines）进行比较：KITTI 相对平移误差达到 0.865%，为最低；在 GEODE 隧道集和 MulRan 上优于基线；在 HeLiPR 上平均结果领先。该方法在四种不同传感器类型上无需重新调参即可泛化。此外实验还实证证明笔直均匀隧道沿其轴向对纯激光雷达配准不可观，验证了方法的机理分析与物理直觉。实验重点涵盖退化场景（隧道/走廊）的定位精度与通用场景的游程质量。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是：揭示了体素化 GICP 中 GN Hessian 掩盖平移退化的机理，提出参数无关的退化感知配准方法 LF-GICP，通过无正则化局域化场与两种统计量（f₀、λ₀）结合时间中值门控与 Fisher 信息加权，在多种数据集和四种传感器上达到最优或领先精度且无需调参。这在即时定位、退化鲁棒性和部署通用性上具有显著意义。局限在于其适用于基于归因方法的 Lidar 配准环节，跨模态（如视觉或事件相机融合）与该框架的结合有待探索；参数无关性依赖于固定校准规则的初始选择。

Abstract: Scan-to-map LiDAR odometry drifts unboundedly along the unobservable axes of geometrically degenerate environments like tunnels and corridors, and existing degeneracy handling requires environment-specific parameter tuning. This paper presents a parameter-free approach. We show that in voxelized GICP the Gauss--Newton (GN) Hessian masks translational degeneracy, because covariance regularization keeps the translation block artificially well-conditioned. We bypass this with a regularization-free voxel-normal localizability field and two of its statistics: a normalized fraction $f_0$ detecting directional anisotropy, and an absolute per-voxel mass $λ_0$ distinguishing information absence (tunnels) from dilution (dense open scenes). A temporal-median gate combines both to trigger Fisher-information correspondence weighting. Calibrated once by fixed rules on two short sequences and then frozen, LF-GICP achieves the lowest KITTI relative translation error ($0.865\%$) under an identical evaluation protocol against re-run baselines, outperforms them on GEODE tunnels and MulRan, leads the HeLiPR mean, and generalizes across four sensor types without re-tuning. We further demonstrate empirically that straight, uniform tunnels remain unobservable along their axis for LiDAR-only registration.

</details>
